Scaling laws have guided resource allocation for large-scale training, yet prior work treats total CLIP model size as a single variable, without exploring how the capacity split between encoders impacts downstream performance. Here, we train multiple CLIP models with different vision and text encoder sizes, revealing that for most vision encoders, there is an optimal text encoder size beyond which zero-shot performance degrades---even as total parameter count increases. Exploiting this behavior yields efficient configurations that match the zero-shot performance of the standard ViT-B/16 architecture with up to 55% fewer parameters. We further show that this degradation stems from overfitting induced by the oversized text encoder, and that using modality-specific weight decay coefficients not only recovers but improves performance across all degraded configurations. A geometric analysis reveals a trade-off in which scaling the text encoder improves embedding uniformity but worsens cross-modal alignment; we further show that these metrics are predictive of zero-shot performance. We hope these findings motivate CLIP architectures and training methods that counteract this degradation, a prerequisite for scaling CLIP reliably and efficiently.

</details>


### [35] [SeRV: Semantic-Aligned Residual Vector Quantization for American Sign Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05742)
*Hongyu Wu,Xu Wu,Tianhao Wu,Jiawei Yu,Phuc Nguyen,Jian Liu,Yi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SeRV（语义对齐残差向量量化）分词器，用于美国手语（ASL）生成，学习语义结构化的残差token空间，并结合分层GPT实现从文本到3D ASL动作的语义一致、结构连贯的生成。同时构建了大规模重建3D ASL动作-文本基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASL生成面临两大挑战：有限的配对文本-动作数据，以及难以学习既要精确重建又能从语言输入预测的动作表示。现有方法依赖仅针对重建优化的动作分词器，缺乏来自配对文本的显式语义监督，导致学习到的token难以支持语义一致且细粒度的ASL动作生成。

Method: 提出SeRV（Semantic-Aligned Residual Vector Quantization）语义对齐RVQ分词器，通过结合句子级动作-文本对齐与token级文本条件监督，学习语义结构化的残差token空间。在此基础上，采用分层GPT以粗到细的方式预测残差动作token，生成结构连贯且语义对齐的3D ASL动作。此外，通过从YouTube-ASL视频恢复配对3D动作，构建了大规模重建的3D ASL动作-文本基准。

Result: 在375小时的ASL视频实验上，SeRV在How2Sign和YouTube-ASL数据集上均取得了最先进的姿态精度，并能从文本直接生成语义一致的3D ASL动作。

Conclusion: 通过将语义监督引入残差向量量化分词过程，SeRV有效解决了ASL生成中动作表示语义不一致的问题，证明了语义对齐的tokenizer配合分层自回归生成能够显著提升ASL动作生成的质量和语义一致性。

Abstract: American Sign Language (ASL) generation remains challenging due to limited paired text-ASL motion data and the difficulty of learning motion representations both precise for reconstruction and predictable from linguistic input. Existing methods rely on motion tokenizers optimized for reconstruction, without explicit semantic supervision from paired text. As a result, the learned tokens remain limited in supporting semantically consistent and fine-grained ASL motion generation. To address this limitation, we propose SeRV (Semantic-Aligned Residual Vector Quantization), a semantic-aligned RVQ tokenizer for ASL generation. SeRV learns a semantically structured residual token space by combining sentence-level motion-text alignment with token-level text-conditioned supervision. Building on this tokenizer, a Hierarchical GPT predicts residual motion tokens in a coarse-to-fine manner, generating structurally coherent and semantically aligned 3D ASL motion. We further construct a large-scale reconstructed 3D ASL motion-text benchmark by recovering paired 3D motion from YouTube-ASL videos. Experiments across 375 hours of ASL video show that SeRV achieves state-of-the-art pose accuracy on both How2Sign and YouTube-ASL datasets, while producing semantically consistent 3D ASL motion directly from text.

</details>


### [36] [Gaussian Linear Functional Manifold Method for Massive Point Cloud Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05744)
*Hong Zhao,Tonglin Zhang,Baijian Yang,Jin Wei-Kocsis,Songlin Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GLFM框架，一种物理信息统计模型，利用确定性线性函数基表示地形连续表面，并用各向同性高斯过程建模微尺度漫射激光后向散射。通过SVD秩约简算法实现线性时间参数估计和闭式二次分类，在35.2 km²真实机载LiDAR数据上达到ARI 0.9933，超越四种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂的Wildland-Urban Interface环境中，从海量非结构化机载LiDAR点云重建连续地形曲面仍具挑战性：深度神经网络需要昂贵逐点标注，非参数曲面重建方法缺乏结构可解释性。

Method: 提出高斯线性函数流形（GLFM）框架：用确定性线性函数基表示连续地形表面，同时将微尺度漫射激光后向散射建模为各向同性高斯过程。为避免精确约束最大似然估计的二次计算成本，开发了代数SVD秩约简算法，实现线性时间参数估计与闭式二次分类。

Result: 在35.2 km²真实机载LiDAR数据上评估，GLFM自动过滤地面点并提取形态特征，对实地验证的地面真值达到0.9933的ARI，优于四种领先基线方法，同时保持适度（out-of-core）内存占用。

Conclusion: 该框架为大规模点云分析提供了严谨、可解释且可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous terrain manifolds from massive, unstructured airborne LiDAR point clouds remains challenging in complex Wildland-Urban Interface (WUI) environments, where deep neural networks require costly point-wise annotations and nonparametric surface reconstruction methods often lack structural interpretability. This paper introduces the Gaussian Linear Functional Manifold (GLFM), a physics-informed statistical framework that represents continuous surface topography using deterministic linear functional bases while modeling microscale diffuse laser backscatter as an isotropic Gaussian process. To avoid the quadratic computational cost of exact constrained maximum likelihood estimation, we develop an algebraic singular value decomposition (SVD) rank-reduction algorithm that enables linear-time parameter estimation and closed-form quadric classification. Evaluated on 35.2 km^2 of real-world aerial LiDAR data, GLFM automatically filters ground points and extracts morphological features, achieving an adjusted Rand index (ARI) of 0.9933 against field-verified ground truth and outperforming four leading baselines while maintaining an out-of-core memory footprint. The framework provides a rigorous, interpretable, and scalable foundation for large-scale point cloud analytics.

</details>


### [37] [GeoContext: One Context Ladder, Two Failure Modes in Vision-Language Geolocation: Flat Reliance on User-Provided Location Context and False Confirmation of Location Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05761)
*Yifan Zhang,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoContext，一个视觉地理定位基准，通过分层构建"上下文阶梯"（粗位置提示到精确验证），评估五个视觉语言模型在109个地点、30个城市上的表现，揭示提示距离与定位误差的关系、模型对无上下文基线的依赖，以及地理验证任务中低于150米容差的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉地理定位基准未考虑用户实际会提供的位置上下文信息（如粗略地点提示），导致模型评估与真实使用场景脱节，缺乏对"给定不同粒度位置上下文时模型定位能力"的系统性研究。

Method: 构建GeoContext基准，包含两个互补任务：(1) GeoHint——给定真实但粗略的位置提示进行开放式定位；(2) GeoVerify——二元验证图像是否在声明地点150米范围内。通过按距离和"可参考性"分层附近的参考点来构建"上下文阶梯"，保持图像不变而变化上下文。覆盖109个地点、30个城市，评估五个VLM，生成21,933条GeoHint响应和6,270条GeoVerify响应。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1) 提示重复率在参考性层级间差异仅1.5个百分点、距离带间差异小于3个百分点，但定位误差随提示距离稳定增加；(2) 模型行为强烈依赖无上下文性能：低准确性地点中位定位误差与提示距离之比约1.00，高准确性地点为0.24-0.69；经站点分组偏差校正后，仅一个模型在近邻提示下保持负的准确性估计；(3) GeoVerify中无边模型在刚超150米容差的诱饵上达到d'=1；模型排名在分离敏感性与响应偏差后发生变化，83.8%的误接受以≥0.8置信度报告。

Conclusion: 模型在利用位置上下文方面表现不一致，尤其在不熟悉的场景中几乎未从近距离提示获益；验证任务的判据不足且过度自信；因此需要更细粒度的评估框架来揭示模型在上下文辅助下的真实定位能力。

Abstract: Visual geolocation benchmarks typically ask a model where an image was captured without accounting for the location context that users often provide. We introduce GeoContext, a resource supporting two complementary tasks: GeoHint, open-ended localization given a true but coarse location hint, and GeoVerify, binary verification of whether an image was taken within 150 m of a claimed place. GeoContext constructs a context ladder by stratifying nearby reference points according to distance and referenceability, allowing the image to remain fixed while the supplied context varies. The benchmark covers 109 sites in 30 cities and evaluates five vision-language models using 21,933 GeoHint responses and 6,270 GeoVerify responses.
  Our evaluation reveals three main patterns. First, hint repetition varies by only 1.5 percentage points across referenceability tiers and by less than 3 points across distance bands, while the resulting localization error increases steadily with hint distance. Second, behavior depends strongly on no-context performance: at sites with low no-context accuracy, the median ratio between localization error and hint distance is approximately 1.00, whereas at higher-accuracy sites it ranges from 0.24 to 0.69. After correcting for bias introduced by the site grouping procedure, only one of the five models retains a negative accuracy estimate when given a nearby hint. Third, in GeoVerify, no model reaches d' = 1 for decoys immediately beyond the 150 m tolerance. Model rankings also change when sensitivity is separated from response bias, and 83.8% of false acceptances are reported with confidence of at least 0.8. We release the benchmark, construction pipeline, audit decisions, and scoring code.

</details>


### [38] [AAMBERS-UAV: Acquisition-Aware Multimodal Backbone Evaluation and Ranking for UAV Weedy Rice Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05762)
*Tarek Rahman,Nazim-E-Alam,Md Kishor Morol,Jannatun Noor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出UAV图像语义分割基准在数据划分上的缺陷（图像级随机划分会使同一航次影像同时出现在训练和测试中），提出"采集感知的同测试评估"协议，在WeedyRice-RGBMS-DB数据集上对比了目标采集排除与目标采集暴露两种划分方案，验证了RGB、四波段多光谱及RGB+MS多模态输入在不同骨干网络下的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV图像具有空间和时间相关性，但现有语义分割基准常以图像级随机划分数据，使同一航次的样本同时出现在训练和测试集中，导致模型能力被高估，无法真实反映模型对新采集区域的泛化能力。因此需要一种考虑采集单元的评估协议来正确衡量模型对新调查场景的迁移性能。

Method: 在734样本的WeedyRice-RGBMS-DB数据集上，固定124张图像作为目标采集的测试集，设计两类协议：(1) target-held-out（开发阶段排除目标采集全部图像）与(2) target-exposed（允许目标采集其余图像进入开发集），两者训练/验证/测试数量完全相同。使用SegFormer-B0评估RGB、四波段MS、七通道RGB+MS三种输入，并在两个固定划分种子下比较；同时用固定划分的U-Net/ResNet18进行复现验证，并开展一致性审计与扰动/腐蚀测试评估模态对几何移动和辐射缩放等的敏感性。

Result: 在完全采集排除下RGB表现最强（IoU 0.7317±0.0201），而目标暴露后RGB+MS变最强（0.7822±0.0269）。U-Net/ResNet18复现确认三种输入的暴露增益均为正，但在两种协议下RGB仍是最佳模态。暴露目标采集图像可以在两个评估骨干上提升测量性能，但对模态排序的影响取决于骨干架构。模块审计发现强近序列依赖，损坏测试显示早期融合对RGB-MS空间位移比对中等辐射缩放更敏感。

Conclusion: 采集感知的同测试评估是对多模态UAV基准中普通图像级划分的必要补充，且模态优劣排序受采集暴露状态和骨干架构影响，结论不能简单依赖单一协议推广。

Abstract: UAV image collections contain spatially and temporally related frames, yet semantic-segmentation benchmarks commonly split them at image level. Such splitting can place samples from one acquisition in both model development and testing, obscuring transfer to a genuinely new survey. Using the 734-sample WeedyRice-RGBMS-DB, we fix a 124-image target-acquisition test set and compare two protocols with identical train, validation, and test counts: target-held-out, which excludes the target acquisition from development, and target-exposed, which admits its remaining images. SegFormer-B0 is evaluated with RGB, four-band multispectral (MS), and seven-channel RGB+MS input over two fixed-split seeds. RGB is strongest under complete acquisition holdout ($0.7317\pm0.0201$ IoU), whereas RGB+MS becomes strongest after target exposure ($0.7822\pm0.0269$). A fixed-split U-Net/ResNet18 replication confirms positive exposure gains for all three inputs, but retains RGB as the best modality under both protocols. Acquisition exposure therefore increases measured performance across both evaluated backbones, while its effect on modality ranking is architecture-dependent. A supplied-split audit reveals strong near-sequential dependence, and corruption tests show that early fusion is substantially more sensitive to RGB--MS displacement than to moderate radiometric scaling. These results support acquisition-aware same-test evaluation as a necessary complement to ordinary image-level splitting in multimodal UAV benchmarks. The code and supporting the findings of this study will be publicly released upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [39] [MolParser-Mobile: Ultrafast OCSR System for Large-Scale Chemical Literature Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05807)
*Xi Fang,Haocheng Lu,Han Lyu,Chengxiang Luo,Linfeng Zhang,Guolin Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MolParser-Mobile，一个通过AutoML优化的轻量级端到端光学化学结构识别(OCSR)框架，仅含9.98M参数，在单张NVIDIA RTX 4090D GPU上达到每秒1,520个分子的吞吐量，同时保持有竞争力甚至更优的识别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCSR是化学文献挖掘的基础组件，尽管深度学习方法的识别准确率已有显著提升，但推理吞吐量仍是限制其网络级大规模部署的关键瓶颈。

Method: 采用AutoML优化技术设计轻量级端到端OCSR框架MolParser-Mobile，通过自动化机器学习搜索实现模型小型化，使参数量压缩至9.98M。

Result: 模型仅含9.98M参数，在单张NVIDIA RTX 4090D GPU上实现每秒1,520个分子的推理吞吐量，同时在多个基准上保持竞争性甚至更优的识别精度。

Conclusion: MolParser-Mobile在显著提升OCSR推理吞吐量的同时保持了高识别精度，为化学文献挖掘的规模化部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Optical Chemical Structure Recognition (OCSR) is a fundamental component of chemical literature mining, enabling molecular database construction, reaction extraction, and AI-driven scientific discovery. Despite substantial progress in recognition accuracy with recent deep learning-based methods, inference throughput remains a critical bottleneck that limits web-scale deployment. To address this challenge, we propose MolParser-Mobile, an AutoML-optimized lightweight end-to-end OCSR framework. MolParser-Mobile contains only 9.98M parameters, while reaching a throughput of 1,520 molecules per second on a single NVIDIA RTX 4090D GPU. Despite its compact design, it maintains competitive and, on several benchmarks, superior recognition accuracy.

</details>


### [40] [CoRe-SAM3: Conditional Semantic--Visual Reconciliation for SAM3 Crack Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05816)
*Shipeng Liu,Liang Zhao,Dengfeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRe（条件语义-视觉调和）方法，利用SAM3内部语义与视觉表征的功能差异，通过轻量语义校准和零初始化条件残差进行受限预测修正，在五个裂缝数据集上将Crack IoU从62.34%提升至70.47%，clDice从81.98%提升至89.24%，仅引入18.914K可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM3虽具有强开放概念分割能力，但直接应用于裂缝分割时会漏检弱裂缝、激活类似背景区域并产生局部边界误差。已有方法简单融合内部语义与视觉表征，但作者通过诊断发现这种直接组合不能带来一致性增益，因而需要基于两种表征功能差异设计更有效的目标域适配策略。

Method: 论文首先在五个裂缝数据集上系统诊断SAM3内部提示条件化语义表征与原生态视觉表征的功能差异。在此基础上提出CoRe框架：将语义预测作为主要决策路径，通过轻量语义校准调整目标域决策映射；利用空间对齐的原生视觉证据生成零初始化、有界且正则化的条件残差，选择性地修正已有预测结果。

Result: 在五个域上，CoRe-SAM3将平均Crack IoU从62.34%提升至70.47%（+8.13），clDice从81.98%提升至89.24%（+7.26），仅引入18.914K可训练参数。预测-转变分析显示CoRe平均纠正34.38%的原生错误，同时仅对SAM3正确分类像素造成0.23%的损伤率。

Conclusion: 研究证实，基于内部表征功能差异的受限预测修正是针对具有强任务先验的视觉基础模型的一种有效且参数高效的目标域适配策略，为后续此类模型的下游微调提供了新思路。

Abstract: Crack segmentation requires a model to recognize target semantics while accurately recovering thin, low-contrast, and topologically continuous local structures. Although SAM3 provides strong open-concept segmentation, its direct application to the crack domain still misses weak cracks, activates crack-like background regions, and produces local boundary errors. We first diagnose the functional differences between the internal prompt-conditioned semantic representation and native visual representation of SAM3 on five crack datasets. The results show that the semantic representation already carries most task information for crack prediction, whereas the utility of the visual representation depends on the current semantic state. Directly combining the two representations does not yield consistent gains. Based on this finding, we propose Conditional Semantic--Visual Reconciliation, termed CoRe. CoRe retains semantic prediction as the primary decision path, applies lightweight semantic calibration to adjust the target-domain decision mapping, and uses spatially aligned native visual evidence to generate a zero-initialized, bounded, and regularized conditional residual that selectively corrects existing predictions. Across five domains, CoRe-SAM3 improves the average Crack IoU from 62.34% to 70.47% and clDice from 81.98% to 89.24%, while introducing only 18.914 K trainable parameters. Prediction-transition analysis further shows that CoRe corrects an average of 34.38% of native errors, with a damage rate of only 0.23% on pixels correctly classified by native SAM3. These results demonstrate that constrained prediction correction based on the functional differences between internal representations provides an effective and parameter-efficient target-domain adaptation strategy for vision foundation models with strong task-specific semantic priors.

</details>


### [41] [Map the Possibilities: Spatial Belief Fields for Language-Goal Aerial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05841)
*Haotian Xu,Yue Hu,Zhengqiu Zhu,Yong Zhao,Wenguan Wang,Quanjun Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 SBFNav，一种基于语言条件空间信念场（SBF）的闭环导航框架，在地面真值未知或目标不可见的情况下，通过维护目标位置的分布表示而非单点预测，在较大尺度连续环境中完成语言引导的空中导航，并在 CityNav 基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言引导的空中导航方法通常将语言接地（language grounding）简化到单一航点或动作，过早地将不完整证据和模糊关系所固有的空间不确定性折叠为确定性预测，导致在目标不可见或关系指令含糊时导航效果不佳。

Method: 提出 SBFNav 闭环导航框架，核心是语言条件空间信念场（Spatial Belief Field, SBF）。SBF 不同于仅记录已观测内容的以自我为中心的（ego-centric）地图，而是表示一个任务条件下合理目标位置的概率分布，在部分证据下保留多个空间假设。每步利用累积观测更新该分布，并选择与指令和观测最一致的目标作为度量航点用于控制。

Result: 在 CityNav 原始和修订版本基准上均取得当前最优整体性能。在 Test Unseen 测试集上，成功率（SR）从 25.91% 提升到 32.29%，SPL 从 19.63% 提升到 30.43%。消融实验验证了空间信念建模相比单点预测的优越性。

Conclusion: 通过以信念分布而非确定性单点来表示语言条件下的目标不确定性，SBFNav 能够在部分观测场景中更好地保留并利用空间假设，从而显著提升大规模连续环境中语言目标导航的准确性和效率。

Abstract: Language-goal aerial navigation requires an agent to local- ize a potentially unobserved target from relational instruc- tions and partial observations, and translate this inference into metric actions in large-scale continuous environments. Existing methods often reduce language grounding to one single waypoint or action, prematurely collapsing the spatial uncertainty inherent in incomplete evidence and ambiguous relations. To address this limitation, we introduce SBFNav, a closed-loop navigation framework centered on a language- conditioned Spatial Belief Field (SBF). Unlike ego-centric maps that primarily record what has been observed, SBF rep- resents a task-conditioned distribution over plausible target locations, preserving multiple spatial hypotheses under par- tial evidence. At each step, this distribution is updated from accumulated observations as new evidence becomes avail- able. Built on this representation, SBFNav selects the goal that best aligns with the instruction and observations as a met- ric waypoint for control. Experiments on both the original and revised CityNav benchmarks achieve the best reported overall performance. On the Test Unseen split, our method improves SR from 25.91% to 32.29% and SPL from 19.63% to 30.43%. Ablation studies further confirm the advantages of spatial-belief modeling over single-point prediction.

</details>


### [42] [Hierarchical Prompt Injector for Domain Generalization Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05864)
*Xin Kun Lin,Ruoyu Guo,Jiaqi Guo,Maurice Pagnucco,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出空间层次提示（SHP）和层次提示注入器（HPI），用于解决域泛化语义分割中提示学习缺乏空间适应性的问题。SHP为每个类别编码多种视角下的区域级几何锚点，HPI实现对提示的空间自适应注入，同时在合成到真实及真实到真实的基准上分别达到70.62%和72.74%的mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域泛化语义分割中，视觉模型常依赖跨域变化的低级外观线索，而结构属性具有跨域稳定性。现有基于提示学习的方法将每个类别编码为单一的全局提示，且对所有像素均匀施加提示，无法适应因视角变化、遮挡和环境变化导致的部分目标区域可见的情况。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：(1) 空间层次提示（SHP），为每个类别丰富多区域级几何锚点，从不同视角捕捉结构外观以实现任意视角下的互补覆盖；(2) 层次提示注入器（HPI），能够对基础模型进行空间自适应的提示注入，通过对提示的语义相关性和空间影响进行建模来实现空间定位，并引入辅助监督使层次提示与对应目标区域对齐。

Result: 在合成到真实和真实到真实的两类域泛化基准上分别达到70.62%和72.74%的mIoU，代码和检查点已公开。

Conclusion: 通过空间层次化的提示设计和空间自适应的提示注入机制，能够有效增强域泛化语义分割模型对结构先验的利用，解决单一全局提示在部分目标区域可见场景下的局限性。

Abstract: Domain Generalized Semantic Segmentation (DGSS) is a challenging task, as vision models often rely on low-level appearance cues that change across domains. In contrast, structural attributes exhibit cross-domain stability, motivating the use of structural priors for DGSS. Existing methods use prompt learning to transfer such priors into DGSS models, but typically encode each class as a single holistic prompt. Moreover, these methods apply prompts uniformly to all pixels, offering no mechanism to adapt when only a subset of object regions is visible due to viewpoint changes, occlusion, and environmental variation. We address this with \textbf{Spatial Hierarchical Prompts (SHP)} that enrich each class with region-level geometric anchors capturing structural appearance from distinct viewing angles, ensuring complementary coverage under arbitrary viewpoints. Additionally, we propose the \textbf{Hierarchical Prompt Injector (HPI)}, which enables spatially adaptive prompt injection in foundation models. HPI spatially grounds prompts by modeling their semantic relevance and spatial influence with visual features. Considering the difficulty of learning spatially and semantically aware prompt injection, we further introduce auxiliary supervision to align hierarchical prompts with their corresponding object regions. We achieve 70.62\% and 72.74\% mIoU on synthetic-to-real and real-to-real benchmarks, respectively. Code and checkpoints are released at https://github.com/MosukFate/HPI

</details>


### [43] [FACT: A Forensic Agent with Compiled Tool-Use Trajectories for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05876)
*Jiaoyang Chen,Bin Hu,Jingyu Hu,Kun Zhou,Qin Zhang,Zhengzhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FACT（具有编译工具使用轨迹的法证代理），一种用于AI生成图像检测的可图像条件工具使用策略。不同于固定检测器，FACT动态决定调用哪些取证工具、解释返回证据并在足够证据时停止，通过Evolve-Distill-Refine流水线实现，在多个基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器通常依赖固定的取证线索集合，导致对某一生成器家族有效的检测器可能在另一家族上失效。随着开放世界中新型图像生成器不断出现、视觉伪影越来越难以识别，需要一种能根据输入图像自适应选择检测工具的方法。

Method: FACT采用Evolve-Distill-Refine三阶段流水线：(1) Evolve：演化经执行验证的取证技能；(2) Distill：将技能编译为动作-观测工具使用轨迹，并蒸馏为紧凑型代理；(3) Refine：使用成本感知的GRPO优化策略。FACT学习图像条件工具使用策略，决定调用哪些取证工具、解释证据并在充分时停止。

Result: 在两个内部基准和四个公开基准上，FACT在所有对比方法中取得最佳性能，包括对近期未见过的生成器、深度伪造和篡改图像均表现优异。

Conclusion: FACT通过自适应工具选择和证据聚合取代固定检测流程，在开放世界中有效提升AI生成图像检测的泛化能力，尤其对未见过的生成器家族表现出鲁棒性。

Abstract: AI-generated image detection is increasingly open-world: new image generators produce highly realistic images that make visual artifacts harder to identify. Existing detectors usually rely on a fixed set of forensic cues, so a detector that works well for one generator family may fail on another. We introduce FACT (Forensic Agent with Compiled Tool-use Trajectories), which learns an image-conditioned tool-use policy for forensic analysis. Instead of applying a fixed detector, FACT decides which forensic tools to call, interprets the returned evidence, and stops when sufficient evidence has been collected. FACT follows an Evolve--Distill--Refine pipeline: it evolves an execution-verified forensic skill, compiles the skill into action--observation tool-use trajectories, distills them into a compact agent, and refines the policy with cost-aware GRPO. Across two internal and four public benchmarks, FACT achieves the best performance among all compared methods, including on recent unseen generators, deepfakes, and manipulated images.

</details>


### [44] [UniFusion: Sparse-View 4D Reconstruction via Unified Spatio-temporal Depth Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05888)
*Yongzhe Lyu,Shaofei Wang,Yixin Chen,Siyuan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的时空深度对齐框架，通过时空神经场表示解决稀疏视角视频中跨视角与跨时间的单目深度不一致问题，并利用对齐后的深度图初始化和监督高斯泼溅模型，实现高质量4D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角视频的4D重建通常依赖单目深度估计提供先验，但由于有限的跨视角重叠和时序变化，单目深度预测在视角和时间上不一致。现有方法分阶段对齐空间和时间维度，需要前景分割掩码，且未能利用时序线索进行跨视角对齐，导致重建质量受限。

Method: 提出统一的时空深度对齐框架：(1) 将不同视角和时间的深度图表示为一组时空神经场，联合解决跨视角和跨时间的不一致性，无需区分前景/背景，也无需依赖外部分割或跟踪模型；(2) 利用神经场表示获得快速收敛并隐式捕获时空相关性；(3) 提出多视角深度顺序损失，并利用经典的尺度和平移不变损失进一步提升深度质量；(4) 对齐后的深度图用于初始化和监督高斯泼溅模型以完成4D重建。

Result: 在Ego-Exo4D和EgoHuman数据集上的实验表明，所提出的深度对齐方法显著提升了基于动态高斯泼溅的重建方法在新视角/新时间合成以及几何精度和一致性方面的表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一时空深度对齐框架有效解决了稀疏视角视频场景下跨视角与跨时间深度不一致的问题，无需前景分割等额外先验，能够作为动态高斯泼溅重建方法的有效前置模块，显著提升4D重建质量。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the challenging problem of 4D reconstruction from sparse-view videos. This setup usually relies on monocular depth estimation to provide priors for the reconstruction model. A key challenge arises from limited cross-view overlap and temporal variation, making monocular depth predictions inconsistent across views and time. Existing methods align spatial and temporal dimensions in separate stages, requiring foreground segmentation masks while failing to leverage temporal cues for cross-view alignment. Contrary to these methods, we propose a unified spatial-temporal depth alignment framework that jointly resolves cross-view and cross-time inconsistencies without distinguishing foreground/background. Our method represents depth maps across views and time as a set of spatio-temporal neural fields. This representation not only yields fast convergence, but also captures spatio-temporal correlation among depth maps implicitly, without dependence on external segmentation/tracking models. We also propose a multi-view depth-order loss while leveraging the classic scale-and-shift-invariant loss to further improve the final depth quality. The aligned depths initialize and supervise Gaussian splatting models for 4D reconstruction. Experiments on Ego-Exo4D and EgoHuman demonstrate that our improved depth alignment substantially benefits dynamic Gaussian-splatting-based reconstruction methods for novel-time/view synthesis and geometry accuracy/consistency.

</details>


### [45] [GenPuzzle: Benchmarking Visual Reasoning in Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05902)
*Changpeng Zhao,Yiren Song,Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GenPuzzle基准，用于评估图像生成系统的视觉问题求解能力。该基准包含2005个问题、12个类别，涵盖模式补全、空间构建、迷宫、数独、拼图、棋盘游戏、数学视觉证明等推理型图像生成任务。评估结果显示最强模型仅达到40.57%的总体准确率，揭示当前系统在逻辑推理、几何理解和状态保持方面的严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成系统虽整合了多模态理解、推理与合成能力，但已有评估集中在美学、提示对齐、组合性或基于文本的答案上，无法判断这些系统是否真正具备视觉问题求解能力并以像素形式忠实表达解。亟需一个以推理为核心的图像生成基准来填补这一评估空白。

Method: GenPuzzle包含2005个问题、12个任务类别（如模式补全、迷宫、数独、非ograms、七巧板、棋盘游戏、火柴棒谜题、正交投影及数学视觉证明）。每个任务提供视觉谜题并需要输出保持输入状态的同时执行逻辑有效解的图像。评估采用任务特定协议：离散网格输出经转录后用程序化验证；视觉复杂输出用分层的、多维度的或二进制的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）评分规则评估，并通过与人类参考分数的一致性来选择自动评判器。

Result: 在三个前沿生成器中，最强模型仅达到40.57%的Macro Overall准确率，在逻辑、几何、状态保持和指令执行方面表现出频繁失败。

Conclusion: GenPuzzle为衡量从图像渲染进步到视觉问题求解提供了测试平台，表明当前图像生成系统距离真正的视觉推理能力仍有显著差距。

Abstract: Recent image generation systems increasingly combine multimodal understanding, reasoning, and synthesis, suggesting that they may do more than render plausible scenes. Yet existing evaluations emphasize aesthetics, prompt alignment, compositionality, or text-based answers, leaving unclear whether these systems can solve visual problems and faithfully express solutions in pixels. We introduce GenPuzzle, a benchmark for reasoning-centric image generation. GenPuzzle contains 2,005 problems across 12 tracks, spanning pattern completion, spatial construction, mazes, Sudoku, nonograms, tangrams, board games, matchstick puzzles, orthographic projection, and mathematical visual proof. Each task provides a visual puzzle and requires an image output that preserves the input state while executing a logically valid solution. GenPuzzle uses task-specific evaluation protocols: discrete grid outputs are transcribed and verified programmatically, while visually complex outputs are assessed with tiered, multidimensional, or binary multimodal large language model (MLLM) rubrics. We further select the automatic judge by measuring agreement with human reference scores. Across three frontier generators, the strongest model reaches only 40.57 Macro Overall, revealing frequent failures in logic, geometry, state preservation, and instruction execution. GenPuzzle provides a testbed for measuring progress from image rendering toward visual problem solving.

</details>


### [46] [FreeTransformSR: Efficient Lightweight Image Super-Resolution via Free Low-Rank Learnable Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05912)
*Hongji Li,Yunhui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeTransformSR是一种基于通道级自由低秩可学习变换的轻量级单图像超分辨率网络，通过任务自适应基函数、局部特征调制分支、软复杂度自适应模块和自适应强度调制策略，在仅595K参数的情况下于五个基准数据集上取得了具有竞争力的PSNR/SSIM性能，并具有更快的推理速度，适合资源受限场景部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图像超分辨率重建中，现有方法在重建质量与模型复杂度之间存在权衡，轻量级模型往往难以充分恢复高频细节，而高性能模型参数和计算量过大，难以部署于资源受限场景。

Method: 提出基于通道级自由低秩可学习变换的轻量级SR网络。核心组件包括：(1) 自由低秩可学习变换，以数据驱动方式学习任务自适应基函数，实现最小参数开销下的自适应特征调制；(2) 局部特征调制分支，利用深度卷积补充变换域处理，增强高频细节恢复；(3) 软复杂度自适应模块，通过轻量级门控网络动态融合局部卷积与窗口自注意力分支的输出，根据区域纹理特征自适应调整融合比例；(4) 自适应强度调制策略，在样本级别调整变换域响应强度，使网络能根据输入特征动态调节处理强度。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的实验表明，FreeTransformSR在显著更少的参数和FLOPs下取得了具有竞争力的PSNR/SSIM性能。具体而言，在BSD100 x2上达到32.41 dB，在Urban100 x4上达到27.00 dB，仅需595K参数，且推理速度优于对比方法。

Conclusion: FreeTransformSR通过轻量级可学习变换与多分支自适应调制机制的结合，在极低参数和计算开销下实现了具有竞争力的超分辨率重建质量，证明了该方法在资源受限场景下高效部署的可行性。

Abstract: Single image super-resolution aims to reconstruct high-resolution images from low-resolution inputs. This paper proposes FreeTransformSR, a novel lightweight super-resolution network based on a channel-wise free low-rank learnable transform. The transform learns task-adaptive basis functions in a data-driven manner, enabling adaptive feature modulation with minimal parameter overhead. To further enhance high-frequency detail recovery, we introduce a local feature modulation branch that complements transform-domain processing with depthwise convolution. In addition, a soft complexity adaptive module dynamically fuses the outputs of local convolution and window self-attention branches through a lightweight gating network, adaptively adjusting the fusion ratio based on regional texture characteristics. An adaptive intensity modulation strategy is also incorporated to adjust transform-domain response strength at the sample level, enabling the network to dynamically adjust processing intensity according to input features. Extensive experiments on five benchmark datasets demonstrate that FreeTransformSR achieves competitive PSNR/SSIM performance with significantly fewer parameters and FLOPs. Specifically, FreeTransformSR achieves 32.41 dB on BSD100 x2 and 27.00 dB on Urban100 x4 with only 595K parameters, while delivering faster inference speed than competing methods, making it well-suited for deployment in resource-constrained scenarios. Source code is available at: https://github.com/HJiLi/FreeTransformSR.

</details>


### [47] [STAR-Pro: Stage-Wise Token Adaptive Reduction with Progressive Refinement for Efficient Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05916)
*Yichen Guo,Tinghao Wang,Qizhe Zhang,Lingbei Meng,Yuan Zhang,Jiajun Cao,Hao Jiang,Chenwei Wu,Jixian Wu,Sixiang Chen,Tao Luo,Hongyang Cheng,Kai Tang,Chenxi Li,Renyuan Li,Xiande Huang,Wenya Wang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR-Pro，一种免训练的两阶段视觉token剪枝框架，用于缓解大型视觉-语言模型(LVLM)因处理数百至数千个视觉token而产生的计算开销。作者通过两种互补性分析发现：融合前的激进剪枝会丢弃大量视觉信息，且解码层中文本到视觉的重要注意力token随层深不断变化，使一次性剪枝决策不可靠。STAR-Pro的Adaptive Stage通过pivoted QR构建超出预算的视觉特征覆盖候选池，Progressive Stage则利用渐进变化的文本-视觉注意力在选定的解码层逐步修剪嵌套的保留集合。在七个LVLM和18个图像/视频基准上验证了方法的有效性，在LLaVA-Video-7B上削减90.5%视觉token的同时保留92.7%的基线与2.24倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM在处理输入时会生成数百至数千个视觉token，带来巨大的计算开销。虽然已有训练无关的视觉token剪枝方法，但作者通过实证分析发现两个关键问题：其一，在跨模态融合前进行激进的一次性剪枝会严重丢失视觉特征空间覆盖信息；其二，跨解码层的文本到视觉注意力分布变化剧烈，表明重要的视觉token在融合过程中是动态演化的，这使固定的一次性剪枝决策不可靠。因此需要设计一种既能保留前期广泛视觉覆盖又能在融合中随注意力信号逐步细化的剪枝策略。

Method: STAR-Pro采用免训练的两阶段框架。(1) Adaptive Stage（自适应阶段）：利用pivoted QR分解对视觉token进行选择，构建一个超出目标预算的特征覆盖候选池，确保在跨模态融合之前保留足够广泛的视觉信息。(2) Progressive Stage（渐进阶段）：在选定的解码器层，利用不断演化的文本到视觉注意力信号对候选池中的token进行嵌套式逐步剪枝，生成逐层递减的survivor集合并满足目标层的平均token预算约束（即layer-average token budget）。整个流程无需任何训练。

Result: 在七个不同架构的LVLM及18个图像与视频基准上的广泛实验证实了STAR-Pro在激进剪枝下的有效性。在LLaVA-Video-7B上，STAR-Pro可将视觉token削减90.5%，同时保留基线92.7%的性能，并实现2.24倍的实测推理加速。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 二元分析揭示了视觉token剪枝中的两个关键洞察：融合前的视觉信息覆盖应保持广泛（过度剪枝会丢失重要视觉细节），且跨模态融合期间的重要token随解码深度动态变化（一次性剪枝过于粗糙）。据此提出了阶段自适应、渐进细化的训练无关剪枝框架STAR-Pro，在极端剪枝下仍能很好地平衡性能与推理速度，为LVLM的高效推理提供了有力方案。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) achieve strong multimodal understanding, but the hundreds to thousands of visual tokens they process impose substantial computational overhead, motivating training-free visual token pruning. In this work, we conduct two complementary analyses of visual token pruning. First, we measure the feature-space coverage of tokens retained before cross-modal fusion and find that aggressive pruning discards substantial visual information. Second, we track text-to-visual attention across decoder layers and find that the visual tokens considered important change substantially with depth, making one-shot pruning decisions unreliable. Together, these findings show that effective pruning should preserve broad visual coverage before fusion and progressively refine the retained tokens as cross-modal evidence evolves during fusion. We therefore propose STAR-Pro (STage-Wise Adaptive Token Reduction with Progressive Refinement), a training-free two-stage framework. Its Adaptive Stage applies pivoted QR to construct an over-budget feature-coverage candidate pool, while its Progressive Stage uses evolving text-to-visual attention at selected decoder layers to prune a nested survivor set under a target layer-average token budget. Extensive experiments across seven LVLMs spanning multiple architectures and 18 image and video benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of STAR-Pro under aggressive pruning. On LLaVA-Video-7B, STAR-Pro reduces visual tokens by 90.5%, retains 92.7% of baseline performance, and achieves a $2.24\times$ measured inference speedup. Code is available at https://github.com/EasonAI-5589/starpro.

</details>


### [48] [PAI-Actor: Cinematic Multi-Character Replacement in Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05918)
*Bangxun Tang,Heyuan Gao,Yiren Song,Guian Fang,Zijian He,Jie Yang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PAI-Actor是一个电影级多角色动画框架，用于在真实视频片段中替换并动画化多个角色，同时保持原始场景动态、相机运动和背景内容。它通过结构引导的人类恢复问题公式化多角色动画，利用电影数据构建训练管线，并提出双向到自回归的蒸馏框架——先训练双向扩散Transformer生成1080P高清短片段，再蒸馏为自回归视频到视频模型以支持高效推理和长视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动画系统主要驱动单一静态图像或单一主体，无法满足电影制作中在真实视频片段中替换和动画化多个角色的需求。这一任务极具挑战性，因为生成的多个角色必须在运动、交互上与源表演保持一致，同时还需匹配背景的光照、阴影、构图和整体电影感外观。

Method: 1）将多角色动画公式化为结构引导的人类恢复问题；2）从高质量电影数据构建电影驱动的训练管线；3）提出双向到自回归（bidirectional-to-autoregressive）蒸馏框架——先训练双向扩散Transformer用于1080P短片段高质量生成，再蒸馏为自回归视频到视频模型，以支持高效推理和更长的视频生成。

Result: 实验表明PAI-Actor实现了高保真度的多角色动画，具备强场景一致性、电影级视觉质量，并支持高效的长视频生成。

Conclusion: PAI-Actor成功验证了通过结构引导恢复与双向到自回归蒸馏相结合的技术路线，能够在保持场景动态和电影感的前提下实现多角色替换与动画生成，为影视级AI动画生产提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present PAI-Actor, a cinematic multi-character animation framework for character replacement in dynamic movie scenes. Unlike conventional animation systems that mainly drive a single static image or a single subject, our goal is to replace and animate multiple characters within real video clips while preserving the original scene dynamics, camera motion, and background content. This setting is particularly challenging because the generated characters must remain consistent with the source performance in motion and interaction, while also matching the surrounding background in lighting, shadow, composition, and overall cinematic appearance. To address this, we formulate multi-character animation as a structure-guided human recovery problem and build a movie-driven training pipeline from high-quality film data. Furthermore, to support practical cinematic production, we introduce a bidirectional-to-autoregressive distillation framework: we first train a bidirectional diffusion transformer for high-quality short-clip generation at 1080P resolution, and then distill it into an autoregressive video-to-video model for efficient inference and longer video generation. Experiments show that PAI-Actor enables high-fidelity multi-character animation with strong scene consistency, cinematic visual quality, and efficient long-form generation.

</details>


### [49] [AVSplat: Dense-View Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting with Assist-View Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05925)
*Muyu Xu,Fangneng Zhan,Yu Wei,Hanspeter Pfister,Shijian Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AVSplat 是一种无位姿的端到端前馈3D高斯泼溅框架，通过"辅助视图前置"（每个视角仅与少量相关且多样的辅助视图进行轻量交互，获得聚焦的场景上下文）和"自适应温度感知体素融合"（在高占用区域锐化归属，由占用率和点置信度引导）两大模块，将额外视角转化为可靠信号，从而恢复正向视角缩放——即增加输入视角数时性能保持稳定甚至提升，而非在稠密视角区间退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无位姿前馈3D高斯泼溅方法在处理稠密多视角输入时性能反而退化，原因有二：全局注意力将所有token扩散到多个视角导致注意力分散；朴素体素融合将大量高斯平均化为过度平滑的表达。这使得"更多视角应带来更好性能"这一直觉无法实现，因此需要将额外视角从干扰信号转化为可靠的增强信号。

Method: AVSplat包含两大核心机制：1) Assist View Preconditioning——在全局注意力之前，每个视角仅与一小撮按相关性与多样性选取的"辅助视角"进行单次轻量交互，利用缓存特征提供聚焦的场景上下文以稳定对应关系；2) Occupancy-guided Voxel Fusion（自适应温度感知体素融合）——在高占用区域动态锐化归属权重，并由占用率和点置信度共同引导融合过程，避免过度平均导致的表达模糊。

Result: 实验证明AVSplat实现了正向视角缩放：随着输入视角数量的增加，性能保持稳定或持续提升，而非像已有方法那样在稠密视角区间退化。消融实验显示，Assist View Preconditioning是防止稠密视角性能退化的主要机制，而Occupancy-guided Voxel Fusion则贡献了大部分单点图像质量提升。

Conclusion: AVSplat有效解决了稠密视角输入下前馈3D高斯泼溅性能退化的问题，通过聚焦的辅助视角交互和占用引导的自适应融合，将更多视角转化为可靠信号，在稳定性和图像质量两方面都取得了提升。

Abstract: Pose-free feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting enables novel view synthesis from uncalibrated multi-view images. Although more views should improve performance, existing methods often degrade with dense-view inputs because global aggregation spreads attention over many tokens, and naive voxel fusion averages many Gaussians into overly smooth representations. We present AVSplat, a framework that turns additional views into reliable signals for both aggregation and representation. Before global attention, each view performs a single lightweight interaction with a small set of Assist Views chosen for relevance and diversity, and the cached features provide a focused scene context that stabilizes correspondence. For representation, we use adaptive temperature-aware voxel fusion that sharpens attribution under high occupancy, guided by occupancy and point confidence. Crucially, AVSplat restores positive view scaling where performance remains stable or improves as more input views are added, instead of degrading in the dense-view regime. Ablations show that Assist View Preconditioning is primarily responsible for preventing dense-view degradation, while Occupancy-guided Voxel Fusion contributes most of the single-point image-quality gains.

</details>


### [50] [Beyond Classification: Structured Supervision Aligns Visual Evidence with Medical Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05937)
*Hexiang Bai,Hanyang Xu,Xiaoxue Li,Xiaoliang Wu,Shangde Gao,Hongxia Xu,Ke Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了医学图像分析中Vision Transformers (ViTs)的替代预训练范式的可行性，评估了三种结构化监督形式：图自监督拓扑先验、分割密集像素约束和图文跨模态语义对齐。实验发现所有方法均能缓解全局池化瓶颈并将注意力引导至前景病灶区域，其中图文跨模态对齐表现最优，通过嵌入高维诊断逻辑实现精确注意力锚定与抽象推理，在下游临床分类任务中获得超越传统分类基线的准确率和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ViT通过全局图像分类进行预训练容易产生空间坍缩问题，即模型过度依赖背景捷径而非定位关键前景病变区域，这限制了其在医学图像分析中的有效性。因此需要探索能够将视觉证据与精确医学语义对齐的替代性预训练范式。

Method: 系统性地评估三种独立的预训练范式：(1) 基于图的拓扑先验自监督学习；(2) 基于分割的密集像素级约束；(3) 基于图文对的跨模态语义对齐。通过对比实验分析各范式对缓解全局池化瓶颈和引导视觉注意力至前景区域的效果。

Result: 三种结构化监督形式均能有效缓解全局池化瓶颈并将注意力转向前景区域，但图文跨模态对齐表现最佳。该语义丰富的预训练显著增强了模型的特征表示，在下游临床分类微调中取得了优越的准确率，并生成聚焦于真实病理特征的高度可解释注意力图。

Conclusion: 基于图文对齐的语义增强预训练能够从根本上摆脱空间坍缩问题，相较于传统分类基线显著提升了ViT在医学图像下游任务中的性能与可解释性，是一种更具优势的替代性预训练策略。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) have shown immense potential in medical image analysis. However, standard pre-training via global image classification suffers from spatial collapse, where models rely heavily on background shortcuts rather than localising critical foreground lesions. To overcome this limitation and align visual evidence with precise medical semantics, we systematically investigate alternative pre-training paradigms.Specifically, we evaluate three independent forms of structured supervision: topological priors via graph self-supervision, dense pixel-level constraints via segmentation, and cross-modal semantic grounding via image-text pairs. Notably, our empirical analysis reveals that while all three forms of structured supervision successfully alleviate the global pooling bottleneck and steer visual attention towards foreground regions, image-text alignment achieves the most superior performance. By embedding high-dimensional diagnostic logic, the cross-modal approach not only anchors attention on precise visual evidence but also enables profound abstract reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that this semantically enriched pre-training fundamentally enhances the model's feature representation. Consequently, when fine-tuned for downstream clinical classification tasks, our models achieve superior accuracy and yield highly interpretable attention maps focused on true pathological features, vastly outperforming vanilla classification baselines.

</details>


### [51] [ProtoRAG: Prototype-Based Retrieval Augmentation for Few-Shot Fine-Grained Remote Sensing Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05953)
*Jian Wang,Yuxiang Hong,Chufeng Zhou,Chao Pang,Xiaokang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProtoRAG是一个基于原型检索增强的框架，通过外部对象级视觉记忆解耦粗定位与细粒度识别，在遥感小样本细粒度目标检测中显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感图像中的小样本细粒度目标检测（FGOD）极具挑战性，因为有限的标注必须同时支持目标定位和对视觉相似子类别的判别。多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）虽能提供粗粒度目标定位，但缺乏可靠的细粒度识别所需的显式视觉证据。

Method: 提出了ProtoRAG框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 为MLLM配备外部对象级视觉记忆；(2) 判别性原型空间学习（DPSL），通过监督对比学习和原型一致性正则化构建可靠视觉记忆；(3) 不确定性引导的候选约束推理策略，仅对模糊实例调用多模态推理，同时用检索到的候选特定视觉参考增强MLLM。

Result: 在九个少样本设置中一致超越代表性基线，在MAR20、HRSC2016和FAIR1M-2.0上分别比最强基线高14.80、2.27和4.04 mAP50。

Conclusion: ProtoRAG有效地解决了小样本FGOD中定位与细粒度识别的解耦问题，验证了原型检索增强方法在遥感细粒度识别中的有效性。

Abstract: Few-shot fine-grained object detection (FGOD) in remote sensing imagery is challenging because limited annotations must support both object localization and discrimination among visually similar subcategories. Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide strong coarse object localization, they lack explicit visual evidence for reliable fine-grained recognition. To address this limitation, we propose ProtoRAG, a prototype-based retrieval-augmented framework that decouples coarse localization from fine-grained recognition by equipping MLLMs with an external object-level visual memory. To construct a reliable visual memory from limited support samples, we introduce Discriminative Prototype Space Learning (DPSL), which encourages discriminative and prototype-stable representations through supervised contrastive learning and prototype-consistency regularization. We further develop an uncertainty-guided candidate-constrained reasoning strategy that augments MLLMs with retrieved candidate-specific visual references and invokes multimodal reasoning only for ambiguous instances. Extensive experiments show that ProtoRAG consistently surpasses representative baselines in nine few-shot settings, outperforming the strongest baselines by 14.80, 2.27, and 4.04 mAP$_{50}$ on MAR20, HRSC2016, and FAIR1M-2.0, respectively.

</details>


### [52] [STP-BENCH: A Unified Systematic Benchmark for Virtual Spatial Transcriptomics from Histopathology Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05956)
*Youngmin Chung,Ji Hun Ha,Andrew H. Song,Cristina Almagro-Pérez,Chaeyoung Seo,Won Jun Suh,Jeong Won Beom,Kyoung Bin Oh,Eytan Ruppin,Faisal Mahmood,Joo Sang Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STP-BENCH，一个用于虚拟空间转录组学（virtual ST）模型评估的标准化基准平台，涵盖六个癌症类型、两个ST平台、超过30,000个spot和至少15张切片，统一评估21种预测方法，并系统分析基因可恢复性、下游生物学效用及模型鲁棒性。核心发现是统一形态学编码会显著改变此前研究中的模型排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学（ST）成本高昂，促使研究者通过计算方式从H&E染色切片直接预测空间基因表达（virtual ST）。然而现有评估存在严重缺陷：数据集规模小且异质、训练与推理流程不一致、缺乏对生物学可解释性和模型鲁棒性的系统评估，导致难以客观比较不同方法的优劣。

Method: 构建包含6种癌症类型、2个ST平台（Visium和Xenium）的标准化基准，训练集均含超过30,000个spot和至少15张切片。统一使用病理学基础模型作为形态学编码器（当架构允许时），重新实现并评估21种预测方法。评估维度包括：(1)高变异基因的平均预测准确率；(2)哪些基因和基因集可从组织形态学恢复；(3)下游生物学效用（细胞类型反卷积和空间域识别）；(4)领域偏移和数据规模下的模型可靠性。

Result: 关键发现是统一形态学编码后，先前研究中建立的模型排名发生了显著重排，表明架构创新与图像编码方式在以往评估中被混淆。基准提供跨平台、跨癌症类型的系统化比较结果，并公开代码与数据支持可复现性。

Conclusion: STP-BENCH是首个大规模、跨平台标准化的virtual ST基准，揭示了统一形态学编码对公平比较的关键性，为领域提供一个可靠且可复现的评估基础设施，有助于推动virtual ST模型的客观进展和社区共识。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) provides unprecedented insights into tumor heterogeneity by capturing spatially resolved gene expression, yet its high experimental cost hinders large-scale adoption. Consequently, computational approaches that predict spatial gene expression directly from hematoxylin and eosin slides, termed virtual ST, have rapidly emerged. Despite this progress, assessing advances in the field remains difficult due to insufficient benchmarking: prior studies rely on small, heterogeneous datasets, inconsistent training and inference pipelines, and limited evaluation of biological interpretability and model robustness. To address these gaps, we present STP-BENCH, a standardized benchmark for virtual ST models. STP-BENCH comprises six cancer types spanning two ST platforms (Visium and Xenium), with each training dataset containing more than 30,000 spots and at least 15 slides to ensure statistical reliability. We evaluate 21 predictive approaches, re-implemented with a unified pathology foundation model as the morphological encoder when architecturally applicable. Beyond conventional benchmarks that report average predictive accuracy on highly variable genes, we systematically examine which genes and gene sets are recoverable from histomorphology. We further evaluate the downstream biological utility of predicted profiles through cell-type deconvolution and spatial domain identification, and assess model reliability under domain shifts and data scaling. Notably, unified morphological encoding substantially re-orders model rankings established in prior studies, indicating that architectural innovations and image encoding have been conflated in previous evaluations. We publicly release STP-BENCH to support reproducibility and serve as a community benchmark at https://github.com/NEXGEM/STP-Bench.

</details>


### [53] [FineHOI: Part-Aware Dense Representations for Zero-Shot Human-Object Interaction Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05959)
*Francesco Tonini,Lorenzo Vaquero,Mohammad Mahdi Derakhshani,Cees Snoek,Elisa Ricci,Cigdem Beyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FineHOI，一个零样本人-物交互（HOI）检测框架，通过从密集patch级特征显式建模交互，克服现有基于Vision-Language Models方法中全局或检测器中心特征压缩交互线索的问题。该方法引入自适应部位级注意力模块和区域感知交互Transformer，在未见交互上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本HOI检测方法依赖全局或检测器中心特征，这些特征压缩了交互线索，难以保留由局部化空间关系定义的人-物交互细节，从而阻碍了模型在未见过的人-物-谓词组合上的泛化能力。

Method: FineHOI包含两个核心模块：(1) 自适应部位级注意力模块（Adaptive Part-Level Attention），通过无监督聚类将人体和物体分解成语义一致的部位，并根据交互相关性进行重加权；(2) 区域感知交互Transformer（Region-Aware Interaction Transformer），整合部位级与全局特征，输出最终的HOI嵌入表示，用于零样本分类。

Result: 大量实验表明FineHOI在多个基准上一致优于现有零样本HOI方法，尤其在未见过交互类别上取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模密集patch级特征中的局部空间交互关系，FineHOI有效弥补了全局与检测器中心表征在细粒度空间推理上的不足，为零样本HOI检测提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Human-Object Interaction (HOI) detection aims to localize humans and objects in images and classify their interactions. Zero-shot HOI focuses on recognizing interactions that are not observed during training, requiring models to generalize beyond seen verb-object compositions. Recent approaches leverage Vision-Language Models (VLMs), benefiting from rich semantic representations. However, they often rely on global or detector-centric features that compress interaction cues and hinder fine-grained spatial reasoning. To overcome this limitation, we propose FineHOI, a zero-shot HOI framework that explicitly models interactions from dense patch-level features. Our approach is motivated by the observation that human-object interactions are defined by localized spatial relationships, which are not preserved by global and detector-centric representations. To this end, we introduce an Adaptive Part-Level Attention module that decomposes humans and objects into semantically coherent parts via unsupervised clustering, and re-weights them based on their interaction relevance. These representations are then integrated through a Region-Aware Interaction Transformer that integrates part-aware and global features and produces the final HOI embedding. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FineHOI consistently outperforms existing zero-shot HOI methods, achieving particularly strong gains on unseen interactions. Code is available at https://github.com/francescotonini/fine-hoi.

</details>


### [54] [Test-Time Weak-to-Strong Alignment: Transferring Implicit Rewards from Weak to Strong Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05968)
*Xin Xie,Fan Zhang,Dong Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AlignGraft方法，利用一对弱模型（对齐模型与其基座）的隐式对齐信号，在测试时引导一个更大的冻结扩散/流模型采样，无需奖励函数或其梯度，即可实现任务特定和对样本特定的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到图像对齐方法（无论是RL还是偏好优化）都需要对每个checkpoint重复训练，且得到的模型固定于训练时的奖励和强度。测试时对齐方法虽然可以在采样期间引导冻结模型，但现有方法只能从奖励函数本身或其梯度、或单独训练的值函数获取每步信号，这在实际中获取奖励梯度困难且泛化受限。本文旨在改变监督来源，探索能否用模型形式的监督（而非奖励函数）来跨规模传递对齐能力。

Method: AlignGraft使用一个对齐的弱模型及其基座模型作为源对齐对，在采样过程中将该对齐对的"速度差"（velocity difference）添加到更大的冻结且从未微调的目标模型上。关键在于对齐模型在训练时将其奖励隐式编码为逐步的、KL锚定的信号，且这一信号以采样器自身的坐标表达。在共享加噪核下该传输是精确的，测试时无需奖励或其梯度。方法无需调度策略，仅需一个标量来控制对齐强度，且该强度可以外推超越源对齐对的强度。

Result: 在图像和视频流模型（Stable Diffusion 3.5、FLUX和Wan）上的实验表明：该转移提升了大冻结模型在偏好、组合性和文本渲染奖励上的表现；能超越源对齐模型本身的性能；同时以很小的恒定采样开销保留大模型的保真度。大量实验证明在弱模型上的一次对齐运行所产生的监督可被整个模型族在测试时复用。

Conclusion: AlignGraft证明了模型形式的监督可以跨规模传递对齐能力，即以一对弱模型的对齐对作为监督源，实现无需奖励函数梯度的测试时对齐，且这一方法高效灵活、可外推对齐强度，为大模型对齐提供了一种免训练的替代方案。

Abstract: Aligning a text-to-image generation flow model with a reward makes it follow objectives that the training data alone does not provide. Alignment fine-tuning delivers this by reinforcement learning (RL) or preference optimization, but it must be repeated for every checkpoint and returns a model fixed at the reward and strength it was trained with. Test-time alignment instead steers a frozen model during sampling, allowing task-specific and sample-specific guidance. Existing methods obtain this only by drawing the per-step signal from the reward function itself, through its gradient, or through a separately trained value function. We propose changing the supervision source: let a pair of weak models, not a reward function, supply the supervision. A source aligned model, kept together with its base as a source alignment pair, stores its training reward as an implicit, step-wise, KL-anchored signal expressed in the sampler's own coordinates. We explore whether this model-form supervision can cross scale, and show that it does: our method, AlignGraft, aligns a larger, frozen, never-tuned model by adding the pair's velocity difference during sampling. The transport is exact under a shared noising kernel and needs neither the reward nor its gradient at test time. The method has no schedules, only a single scalar that controls the alignment strength and can extrapolate it beyond that of the source alignment pair. Across image and video flow models (Stable Diffusion 3.5, FLUX, and Wan), the transfer lifts the frozen large model on preference, compositional, and text-rendering rewards, can exceed the source aligned model itself, and preserves the large model's fidelity at a small constant sampling overhead. Extensive experiments show that one alignment run on a weak model produces supervision that the whole model family can reuse at test time.

</details>


### [55] [Efficient and Robust Camera-independent Multiview 3D Geometric Reconstruction from Noisy Monocular Depth Estimation and Multiple Point Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05972)
*Marius Leordeanu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅基于点集间相机无关的线性关系进行3D几何重建的方法，通过学习一个线性几何自回归矩阵W来表征3D点结构，该矩阵不依赖世界坐标系或相机位姿，是点集的固有属性。方法有两个版本：一个利用噪声单目深度图进行鲁棒线性估计，另一个在弱透视投影下直接从2D投影估计线性关系。方法不恢复相机位姿，只重建3D结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的3D几何重建方法通常依赖相机投影矩阵和位姿恢复，而现有单目深度估计模型虽然快速但噪声较大。作者希望开发一种不依赖相机参数、仅基于点集内部几何关系的鲁棒重建方法。

Method: 1) 从多帧点对应中学习一个线性几何自回归矩阵W，该矩阵将3D中一个点表示为其他所有点的线性组合；2) 证明W的特征值1对应的主特征向量提供3D点构型的齐次表示；3) 版本一利用深度估计模型生成的噪声单目深度图，通过多帧鲁棒线性估计出W；4) 版本二在弱透视投影情形下，直接从2D投影中估计3D点间的线性组合关系。

Result: 该方法能够仅通过点间的线性关系（对时间稳定、经过多帧鲁棒估计）高效地重建3D结构，且完全不需要使用相机投影矩阵或恢复相机位姿，在学习到的W的特征向量中直接获得3D点构型的表示。

Conclusion: 论文提出一种全新的、不依赖相机参数的3D几何重建范式。通过自学回归矩阵W的固有几何特性来实现仅恢复3D结构的目标，这与传统文献中需要相机姿态和投影模型的3D重建方法形成关键区别。

Abstract: We present an efficient and robust method for 3D geometric reconstruction that is based solely on the camera-independent linear relationships among a given set of points, which are stable over time and robustly estimated using multiple point matches. We essentially learn, from correspondences between points across several frames, a linear geometric auto-regression matrix $\mathbf{W}$, which establishes how a point in 3D can be expressed as a linear combination of all the others. This matrix is constant and does not depend on the world coordinate system or the camera pose---it is an intrinsic property of the point set. We also show that the principal eigenvectors of $\mathbf{W}$, which all have eigenvalue $1$, provide a homogeneous representation of the 3D point configuration.
  The first version of our method takes advantage of noisy monocular depth maps in order to obtain, from multiple frames, a robust geometric auto-regression matrix $\mathbf{W}$ of linear relationships between the 3D points. Thus, we build on recent advances in deep learning, which now provide monocular depth estimation models that are fast but very often noisy. Our approach handles noise through robust linear estimation over several frames.
  The second version of our method does not need monocular depth estimation maps. It applies in cases of weak-perspective projection, when the linear combinations between the 3D points can be robustly estimated from their 2D projections in the image.
  Note that the camera projection matrix is never used in our derivations. Consequently, our method does not recover camera pose, but only 3D structure. This is a key difference between our method and the related literature on 3D geometric reconstruction.

</details>


### [56] [Accelerating Diffusion Transformers with Gaussian Process Rectified Feature Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05981)
*Zhirong Shen,Rui Huang,Chang Zou,Shikang Zheng,Jiacheng Liu,Peiliang Cai,Zhengyi Shi,Yaosong Du,Liang Feng,Xiaobing Tu,Jinkui Ren,Xiantao Zhang,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GP-Refiner框架，利用高斯过程回归对扩散Transformer中基于预测的特征缓存方法进行动态修正。研究发现缓存方法全计算步特征与参考全计算轨迹之间的残差局部服从零均值高斯分布，从而将全计算步特征视为参考特征的带噪观测，解决了加速过程中标签数据不可用的问题。结合GPR的性质，提出不确定性自适应计算策略，通过实时监控后验方差触发必要的全计算校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer在生成式AI中占主导地位，但高计算成本严重阻碍实时应用。基于预测的特征缓存虽被广泛用于加速，但随着步数增加，其预测与参考全计算轨迹之间的偏差逐渐增大。直观想法是用在线回归模型动态修正偏差，但加速过程中标签数据不可用。

Method: 首先提出统计观察：缓存方法全计算步特征与参考全计算轨迹之间的残差局部服从零均值高斯分布。基于此，将全计算步特征视为参考特征的带噪观测，解决标签数据获取问题。进而提出即插即用的GP-Refiner修正框架，采用高斯过程回归进行修正，并利用GPR特性引入不确定性自适应计算策略，通过实时监控后验方差触发必要的全计算校准。

Result: 实验表明，GP-Refiner与多种最先进方法结合时在不同模型上均有显著改进。与TaylorSeer结合时，计算负载降低19.3%，PSNR提升0.9 dB，LPIPS从0.46降至0.29。

Conclusion: GP-Refiner作为一种即插即用框架，能有效修正预测型特征缓存在扩散Transformer加速中的累积偏差，通过高斯过程回归和不确定性感知策略实现显著的效率与质量双重提升，代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers have become the dominant paradigm in generative AI, but their high computational costs severely hinder real-time applications. Prediction-based feature caching is widely used to accelerate diffusion transformers; however, as the number of steps increases, the deviation between its predictions and the reference full-compute trajectory gradually grows. An intuitive idea is to use an online regression model to dynamically correct this deviation, but it faces the issue of label data being unavailable during the acceleration process. This paper presents a statistical observation that the residuals between the features of full computation steps using caching methods and reference full-compute trajectory locally exhibit a zero-mean Gaussian distribution. By treating the features of full computation steps as noisy observations of reference features, the data acquisition problem is resolved. Based on this observation, a plug-and-play GP-Refiner correction framework is proposed. This method utilizes Gaussian Process Regression for correction and, leveraging the properties of GPR, introduces an uncertainty-adaptive computation strategy that triggers necessary full-computation calibration by monitoring the posterior variance in real time. Experiments demonstrate significant improvements across different models when combined with various state-of-the-art methods. Integrating the proposed framework with TaylorSeer reduces the computational load by 19.3% while improving PSNR by 0.9 dB and reducing LPIPS from 0.46 to 0.29. Code is available in https://github.com/Aredstone/GP-Refiner.

</details>


### [57] [MORPHA: Morphology-Constrained Training and the Limits of Cross-Acquisition Transfer in Low-Resource Malaria Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05990)
*Favour Okechukwu Igwezeke,Chikodili Helen Ugwuishiwu,Joseph Uzochukwu Emesiani,Samuel Ifebuche Agada,Ekenechukwu Lilian Anozie,Mary Ofuru Kama,Adaobi Chiazor Emegoakor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文（MORPHA）研究了低资源疟疾显微镜诊断中，基于形态学的训练约束在不同制片技术（薄涂片 vs 厚涂片）间迁移的有效性。作者利用BBBC041数据集推导阶段统计量作为形态一致性约束，在乌干达真实野外图像（Lacuna）上验证：该约束使二分类跨域泛化下降减少30.8%（F1从0.578升至0.699），同时将分布内标定误差降低49%，且测量内容本身而非约束机制贡献了主要增益。文章同时定位了形态学约束的适用边界——即在薄→厚检测任务中统计量不迁移、伪标注跨采集失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低资源疟疾显微镜场景下，模型常在一个制片条件下训练、却在另一种采集条件下部署（如薄涂片→厚涂片），这种采集差距带来的域偏移问题普遍存在。关键问题是：将测量的寄生虫形态学作为训练约束是否能提升跨采集迁移性能，以及何时仅仅使用通用的正则化手段就已足够。目前缺乏对形态学约束在真实非洲野外数据上迁移有效性的系统评估。

Method: 提出MORPHA形态一致性约束：从带分期的BBBC041数据集推导各分期条件下的形态统计量，对偏离这些统计量的预测施加惩罚。该约束不改变架构或推理过程，统一适用于二分类、目标检测和分期感知三种范式，在二分类范式中用于塑造训练过程。研究在乌干达Lacuna真实野外数据上进行薄涂片到厚涂片图像的迁移实验，并设计了使用随机统计量的无内容对照、两种标准置信度正则化器的对比实验，同时绘制两个部署相关边界（薄统计量到厚检测的迁移边界和跨采集伪标注的失败边界）。

Result: MORPHA约束在薄涂片→厚涂片迁移中使二分类泛化下降减少30.8%（F1 0.578→0.699），域内代价可忽略；分布内标定误差降低49%（ECE 0.0162→0.0082）。无内容对照仅恢复25.3%的下降（小于30.8%），证明真实形态学内容贡献主要增益。两种标准置信度正则化器在原始迁移上超过该约束，揭示了形态学约束与通用正则化的适用边界。此外，薄片形态统计量无法迁移到厚片的检测任务（滋养体AP@0.50降至0.000），且跨采集伪标注在过滤生效前即失效。

Conclusion: MORPHA提供了一种基于形态学的一致性约束信号，并给出了低资源疟疾场景下数据集与模型设计的循证指导：形态学约束在二分类跨采集迁移和标定上有效，但必须认识到其适用边界——在检测任务中统计量跨采集不迁移，伪标注策略在跨采集时不可靠，而某些情况下通用正则化即可达到甚至超越形态学约束的效果。

Abstract: In low-resource malaria microscopy, a model trained on one smear preparation routinely meets images from another, and how well morphology-based constraints transfer across this acquisition gap is unclear. We study this on real African field microscopy from Uganda (Lacuna), asking where encoding measured parasite morphology as a training constraint improves cross-acquisition transfer and where generic regularisation suffices. We present MORPHA, a morphological consistency constraint that derives stage-conditional statistics from the stage-annotated BBBC041 dataset and penalises predictions that deviate from them. Defined uniformly across binary, object-level, and stage-aware regimes without changing architecture or inference, it shapes training in the binary regime. The detection regime is a mapped boundary. On transfer from thin-smear cells to thick-smear field images, the constraint reduces the binary-classification generalisation drop by 30.8% (F1 0.578 to 0.699) at negligible within-domain cost and lowers in-distribution calibration error by 49% (ECE 0.0162 to 0.0082). A content-free control applying the identical constraint to random statistics recovers less of the drop (25.3% vs 30.8%), indicating the measured content, not constraining alone, contributes to the gain. Two standard confidence regularisers exceed the constraint on raw transfer, locating where morphology adds value and where generic regularisation suffices. We map two deployment-relevant boundaries: thin-smear statistics do not transfer to thick-smear detection (trophozoite AP@0.50 falls to 0.000), and cross-acquisition pseudo-labelling fails before filtering applies. Together these yield a morphology-grounded consistency signal and evidence-based guidance for malaria dataset and model design in low-resource settings.

</details>

### [58] [When Agent Governance Helps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05531)
*Michael Ray Johnson,Linda Naimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了受治理的自主体AI代理组织规范框架GAMPO。通过对321篇文献的定性证据综合，将代理、敏捷、平台与治理理论整合为可运行的规范；随后在CHI-Bench医疗基准上对不同模型进行提示层实例化测试。核心发现是一个边界条件：治理收益受限于模型的备用容量且具有领域和模型特异性。在容量受限的开源模型上完整流程无可靠收益，而一句"验证你的写入"提示使任务成功率翻倍（pass@1 从2/20升至4/20）。在前沿模型上，用基于案例的自定义"完成定义"（answer-blind、不依赖隐藏答案）替代通用流程，将事先授权任务的性能提升至84%（最佳五次自洽）和68%（单次尝试），利用管理达44%。研究为探索性发现，样本量小（每格n=5-25）且为单次试验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前没有任何规范描述应如何设计与评估在护栏内追求自生成目标的受治理自主AI代理组织。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供一个具名、可审计的框架，并实证探究"治理"本身何时以及为何能带来收益。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：（1）基于321篇文献的文档化定性证据综合（document-based qualitative evidence synthesis），整合代理理论、敏捷方法、平台架构与治理理论，形成GAMPO框架的可运行规范；（2）在CHI-Bench长地平线医疗基准上，对开放模型与前沿模型进行GAMPO的提示层实例化测试，对比通用流程与案例基础的自定义定义完成（answer-blind、基于自身政策与发布标准）在不同子任务上的表现。

Result: 发现治理收益受能力门槛（spare capacity）的调节。容量受限的开源模型上，完整治理流程无可靠增益，而一句"验证你的写入"指令将pass@1从2/20提升至4/20；前沿模型间差异显著：同一脚手架使一个模型的先前授权从24%升至40%，却在另一模型上零增益，且可归因于稳定的推荐覆盖倾向。将通用流程替换为answer-blind、按案例定制的定义完成（不依赖隐藏密钥），在先前授权任务上实现最佳五次自洽84%（单次68%），利用管理44%，但护理管理遭遇主观内容质量壁垒。

Conclusion: 治理应与模型的备用容量相匹配（"sized to spare capacity"）；在前沿模型上，基于案例的规范优于统一流程。贡献在于提出了一个具名可审计的治理框架，并提供证据表明治理效益是能力门槛约束的。但结果为探索性：仅部分实例化、每格样本量小（n=5-25）、单次试验，需要更大规模与跨领域的验证。

Abstract: No specification says how a governed autotelic AI agent organization, where agents pursue self-generated goals inside guardrails, should be designed and evaluated. We answer in two parts. First, we synthesize the Governed Autotelic Multi-Agent Product Organization (GAMPO) framework from a document-based qualitative evidence synthesis of 321 sources, integrating agency, agile, platform, and governance theory into a runnable specification. Second, we probe a prompt-layer instantiation of GAMPO on CHI-Bench, a long-horizon healthcare benchmark, across open and frontier models. The result is a boundary condition: governance benefit is gated by a model's spare capacity and is domain- and model-specific. On capacity-constrained open models the full procedure yields no reliable benefit, whereas a single "verify your writes" sentence doubles task success (pass@1 2/20 to 4/20). At the frontier the same scaffold lifts prior-authorization 24% to 40% but nets zero on another model, a gap traced to a stable recommendation-override disposition. A second result refines the first: replacing the generic procedure with an answer-blind, per-task definition-of-done, keyed only to the case's own policy and published standards, never the hidden key, raises prior-authorization to 84% under best-of-five self-consistency (68% single-attempt, confirmed by a held-out board) and utilization-management to 44%, while care-management meets a subjective content-quality wall. The contribution is a named, auditable framework and capability-gated evidence that governance should be sized to spare capacity, and that at the frontier a case-grounded specification beats a uniform procedure. Findings are exploratory: partial instantiation, small per-cell samples (n = 5-25), and single trials.

</details>


### [59] [TamilEOT: A Dataset and Model for Semantic End-of-Turn Detection in Tamil Telephone Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05631)
*Santhoshkumar V*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布了 TamilEOT：从116段真实泰米尔语电话录音中切出的18,485个带标注的语音轮次边界数据集，以及两个基于Smart Turn v3微调的纯音频端到端（EOT）检测模型。在覆盖30通未见通话的4,168条片段测试中，模型准确率从零样本的70.30%提升至83.71%（8.7MB模型）和86.13%（21MB模型），ROC-AUC从0.751提升至0.921，且单线程在笔记本CPU上运行均低于150ms。论文还公开了构建成本、标注质量审计、消融实验及全部负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音智能体必须在每个停顿处判断用户是否已经说完。缺乏语言模型时只能依赖固定静音超时，设置过短会打断用户，设置过长则每轮对话都付出完整等待代价。虽然存在开放语义的端到端检测器，但目前没有覆盖南印度语言（如泰米尔语）的方案，因此需要构建该语种下的EOT检测资源。

Method: 从116段真实泰米尔语电话对话中切出18,485条标注的轮次边界片段；使用规则从人工转写推导标签，并进行盲听人工核验（正类正确率95.9%，负类44.4%，低于随机水平）；随后改用音频LLM标注器（与人工一致性达97.5%，成本仅5.69美元）。基于Smart Turn v3微调两个纯音频端到端检测器（8.7MB和21MB），并系统测量了编码器容量、种子随机性、VAD与流式适配层对性能的影响。

Result: 在30通未见通话的4,168条片段上，准确率由零样本70.30%升至83.71%（小模型）和86.13%（大模型），ROC-AUC由0.751升至0.921；两模型均可在150ms内于笔记本CPU单线程运行。消融显示只有编码器容量产生实质影响；相同配置同种子三次运行间标准差达0.87个准确率点，构成所有其他差异的噪声下限；生产VAD与流式适配层额外损失2.60点，且7.8%的边界从未被传递给模型。

Conclusion: 作者在泰米尔语上构建并开放了首个端到端语音轮次结束检测数据集和两个高效模型，验证了零样本基线在该语种上的不足，并透明地报告了数据标注中的规则与模型目标错配问题、标注成本、实验噪声下限以及真实系统部署中的性能损耗。数据、权重、代码及全部负面结果均予以公开。

Abstract: A voice agent has to decide, at every pause, whether the user has finished speaking. Without a model of the language that decision falls back to a fixed silence timeout: set it short and the agent interrupts, set it long and every turn pays the full wait. Open semantic end-of-turn detectors exist, but to our knowledge none covers a South Indian language. We release TamilEOT: 18,485 labelled turn boundaries cut from 116 real Tamil telephone conversations, and two audio-only detectors fine-tuned from Smart Turn v3. On a held-out split of 4,168 clips from 30 unseen calls, accuracy rises from 70.30% zero-shot to 83.71% (8.7 MB) and 86.13% (21 MB); ROC-AUC rises from 0.751 to 0.921. Both models run in under 150 ms single-threaded on a laptop CPU. We also report what building it cost. Rule-derived labels, checked against a blind human listening pass, were right 95.9% of the time on the positive class and 44.4% on the negative class, which is below chance, because the rule answered a different question than the model is asked. Replacing them with an audio-LLM labeller measured at 97.5% human agreement cost US$5.69. Of every training lever we measured, only encoder capacity moved the result; three runs at identical config and seed span 0.87 accuracy points, which is the floor below which none of our other deltas mean anything. Replaying the same labelled boundaries through the production VAD and streaming adapter costs a further 2.60 points, and 7.8% of boundaries are never surfaced to the model at all. Data, weights, code and every negative result are public.

</details>


### [60] [Better Together: Complementary Query Rewriting Under a Strong RAG Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05637)
*Sara Shanian,Xiaoqin Yi,Pavlo Ruban,Kurt MacDonald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了RAG中查询改写（Query Rewriting）策略在强检索管线下的真实收益，发现单一改写方法最多与强基线持平，但多种方法组合可通过互补性带来显著提升（企业数据HIT@10提升+12.5至+13.8）。由于改写成本高昂，作者提出一个基于置信度门控的路由器，仅在基线置信度低时才触发改写，可在约40%改写成本下捕获约一半的融合增益（+4.3 HIT@10，+1.92 F1），并在不需要的AmbigNQ数据上自动拒绝改写。核心观点：查询改写应作为一种互补的覆盖来源，通过成本感知路由来使用，而非替代强基线的独立方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为查询改写（将用户问题改写为多个变体再检索）能提升RAG性能，但很少有工作检验：当底层检索已经足够强时，改写是否仍能带来增益。此外，现有工作往往忽视改写方法的计算成本以及不同方法之间的互补性。本文旨在回答上述问题，探究强检索管线背景下单一改写与组合改写的真实效用边界。

Method: 在固定竞争性管线（BGE稠密检索+交叉编码器重排+MMR多样化解重）下，对三种数据集（HotpotQA、AmbigNQ、512K文档的EnterpriseRAG-Bench）进行三种子配对自助显著性检验。比较4种查询改写策略（S1-S4）与2种强LLM基线（HyDE、Query2Doc），评估单一方法、事后整合（union）及各数量方法的组合效果，并通过预算匹配对照组区分互补效应与检索预算效应。设计置信度门控路由器的仿真评估，以基线top-1分数作为触发条件控制改写成本。

Result: 单一改写方法至多与强基线持平；但组合方法产生超额增益。四方法整合（S1+S3+S4+HyDE）在企业数据上将HIT@10从39.22提升至51.70（+12.5），五方法整合达52.98（+13.8）；预算匹配对照仅捕获约40%增益，证实互补性是主导因素。HotpotQA上整合收益+1.6至+1.8（p<0.001），已趋近全方法oracle；AmbigNQ上融合反而有害（低于最佳单方法2.4，p<0.001）。置信度门控路由器以不足40%的查询改写成本捕获企业全融合约一半增益（+4.3 HIT@10），并在AmbigNQ上自动拒绝改写；下游答案质量评估中F1提升+1.92（p<0.01）。

Conclusion: 查询改写不应被视为强基线的独立替代品，而应被视作一种互补的覆盖率来源。实际应用时应通过成本感知路由实现按需触发，不同改写策略在不同问题上各有失败模式，组合使用才能最大化收益，但需结合路由机制控制推理成本。

Abstract: A popular way to improve Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is to rewrite the user's question into several variants and search with all of them. We test whether this actually helps once the underlying search is already strong. Under one fixed, competitive pipeline (BGE dense retrieval, cross-encoder reranking, and MMR diversification), we compare four query-rewriting strategies (S1-S4) against two strong LLM baselines (HyDE, Query2Doc) on three datasets (HotpotQA, AmbigNQ, and the 512K-document EnterpriseRAG-Bench) over three seeds with paired-bootstrap significance tests. Our headline result is that rewriting alone is at best competitive with a strong baseline, but combining methods yields outsized gains because different strategies fail on different questions. A post-hoc union of four methods (S1+S3+S4+HyDE) improves HIT@10 over the baseline by +12.5 points on enterprise data (51.70 vs 39.22), and a five-method union reaches 52.98 (+13.8). Budget-matched controls capture only ~40% of this gain, confirming that complementarity, not retrieval budget, is the primary driver. On HotpotQA the union adds +1.6 to +1.8 points (p<0.001), saturating the all-method oracle; on AmbigNQ the same fusion hurts (-2.4 below the best solo, p<0.001), and we analyze when and why. Because rewriting is expensive, we evaluate in simulation a confidence-gated router that runs rewriting only when the baseline's own top-1 score is low. It captures about half of the enterprise full-merge gain (+4.3 HIT@10) while paying rewriting cost on <40% of queries, and automatically declines to rewrite on AmbigNQ. A downstream answer-quality evaluation confirms the router improves F1 by +1.92 (p<0.01) at roughly 40% of the expansion cost. In short: treat query rewriting as a complementary coverage source, applied through cost-aware routing, not as a standalone replacement for a strong baseline.

</details>


### [61] [Who Maintains Agent Skills? A Longitudinal Study of Human-Governed, AI-Assisted Skill Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05677)
*Chen Shen,Estevam Hruschka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次从人类维护视角研究终身学习AI智能体的技能策展（skill curation）过程。作者挖掘了五个公共AI技能仓库的完整提交历史（873次提交、143个技能文件、254次实质性后创建编辑），使用预注册的治理、操作和触发证据编码手册进行标注。研究发现：（1）所有实质性编辑均由人类账户创作或合并，62%带有AI共同作者标记，且仓库间差异显著；（2）这些编辑是真正的策展行为，多数改变技能内容，以添加和修正为主；（3）预注册的"规则相似度"轴未能通过可靠性门槛，从提交工件中可靠编码规则相似度仍是开放测量问题。作者发布了语料库、编码手册、挖掘脚本和重放协议，结论是公共技能维护更像"人类治理、AI辅助"的循环而非自主流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能策展自动化研究主要与自动化基线对比，将人类维护视为未测量的瓶颈。本文旨在直接研究该缺失过程，理解现实世界中技能维护的真实模式（谁在做、做什么、如何触发），为自动化技能策展系统提供经验基准和评估锚点。

Method: 采用有目的抽样选取五个公共AI技能仓库，挖掘2025年10月至2026年6月间873次提交、143个技能文件中的254次实质性后创建编辑；使用预注册的治理（治理）、操作（操作）和触发证据三类编码手册对每次编辑进行人工标注；并对代表性样本进行审计验证编辑真实改变了技能内容；同时测试一个预注册的"规则相似度"编码轴的可信度。

Result: 三项主要发现：（1）100%的实质性编辑通过人类账户创作或合并，62%带有AI共同作者trailer，但仓库间差异很大；（2）编辑属于真实策展行为，经审计样本确认多数实质改变了技能内容，操作以添加与修正为主；（3）规则相似度编码轴未能通过信度门槛，说明从提交工件中可靠判断规则相似度仍是未解决问题的测量难题。研究还产出了可复用的公开语料、编码手册、脚本与重放协议。

Conclusion: 在自我进化的AI智能体部署现实中，公共技能维护目前主要表现为"人类治理、AI辅助"的循环，而非自主流水线。未来的自动化技能策展器必须以此人类治理模式为基准，并设计在人类治理框架内运作的机制。同时，规则相似度等更深层代码特征的可靠测量仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Lifelong LLM agents increasingly rely on external skill artifacts as one element for preserving and reusing capabilities over time. These skills (usually portable Markdown files such as SKILL.md) describe when and how to apply a capability and must be corrected, expanded, and consolidated as tools and usage patterns shift over deployment. Recent work seeks to automate skill curation, but it largely evaluates against automated baselines and treats human maintenance as an unmeasured bottleneck. We study that missing process directly. We mine the full commit histories of five public AI-skill repositories, a purposive sample of AI-tooling organizations, covering 873 commits, 143 skill files, and 254 substantive post-creation edits from October 2025 to June 2026. We code each edit with pre-registered governance, operation, and trigger-evidence codebooks. Three findings emerge. First, every substantive edit is authored or merged through a named human account, while 62% carry an AI co-author trailer, with large repository-level variation. Second, these edits are genuine curation: an audited sample shows that most change skill content, and the coded operations are dominated by additions and corrections. Third, a pre-registered rule-likeness axis fails its reliability gate; reliably coding rule-likeness from commit artifacts remains an open measurement problem. We release the corpus, codebooks, mining scripts, and a replay protocol for automated skill curators. For self-evolving agents, public skill maintenance currently looks less like an autonomous pipeline than a human-governed, AI-assisted loop that future curators must measure against and operate within.

</details>


### [62] [A Rubric-Guided Large Language Model Solution for Opioid Use Disorder Computable Phenotyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05682)
*Mengxian Lyu,Paredes Pardo,Cheng Peng,Ziyi Chen,Mengyuan Zhang,Jieting Li Lu,Gary M Reisfield,William M Greene,Jenny Lo-Ciganic,Yonghui Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于规则指导的大语言模型（LLM）框架，结合OPRO优化技术，用于从电子健康记录（EHRs）中识别阿片类药物使用障碍（OUD）。该框架使用18项专家制定的规则指导LLM自动提取关键临床文本证据，在253名患者（68例OUD阳性）的验证中取得了0.774的最佳F1分数和0.934的AUROC，分别比机器学习方法和零样本LLM高出12.8%和44.4%的相对F1提升，同时提供更好的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿片类药物使用障碍是美国的公共卫生危机，但仅依靠电子健康记录中的诊断代码难以准确识别，因为许多支持性证据隐藏在临床叙述中。准确识别OUD对于支持干预和改善健康结果至关重要。

Method: 该研究构建了基于规则指导的大语言模型（LLM）框架，融合了OPRO（Optimization by PROmpting）技术，通过18项由专家制定的评估规则指导LLM自动提取带有支持性证据的关键临床文本，以确定OUD标记。研究由两名UF Health医生对253名患者（含68名OUD阳性病例）进行图表审查作为金标准。

Result: LLM-based可计算表型（CP）取得了最高F1分数0.774和AUROC 0.934，相对F1提升方面，比基于机器学习的CP（使用EHR和NLP提取变量）高12.8%，比零样本LLM高44.4%。

Conclusion: 所提出的LLM-based可计算表型能够将LLM提取的证据与OUD表型判定相连接，从而在提升性能的同时增强了可解释性，为OUD的临床识别提供了更有效、更可信的解决方案。

Abstract: Opioid use disorder (OUD) remains a public health crisis in the United States, yet it is difficult to identify from electronic health records (EHRs) because missing diagnosis codes and supporting evidence are buried in clinical narratives. Accurate OUD identification is critical to support interventions and improve health outcomes. This study developed a rubric-guided large language model (LLM) that incorporated Optimization by PROmpting (OPRO) for OUD computable phenotyping (CP). The framework used an 18-item, expert-identified rubric to instruct LLMs to automatically extract critical text with supporting evidence to determine OUD flags. Two UF Health physicians (GMR and WMG) chart-reviewed 253 patients, including 68 OUD-positive cases. Our LLM-based computable phenotype (CP) achieved the best F1 score of 0.774 and an AUROC of 0.934, outperforming the machine learning-based CP using EHR and natural language processing-extracted variables, and zero-shot LLMs by relative F1 improvements of 12.8% and 44.4%, respectively. The proposed LLM-based CP could link LLM-extracted evidence to OUD phenotyping for better explainability.

</details>


### [63] [Intra-Prompt Parallel Decoding for Common-Context Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05707)
*Theodore Glavas,Nikhita Vedula,Dushyanta Dhyani,Antonios Valkanas,Yilun Zhu,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Intra-Prompt Parallel Decoding (IPPD)的推理方法，通过在单个prompt内并行解码多个共享上下文的问答任务，利用虚拟位置ID和注意力掩码操作实现与标准prompting相同的输出，无需微调或架构改动，最高可提升7倍有效吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在CCQA任务中，多个问题共享同一上下文，但LLM通常以独立prompt逐个生成答案。现有的批处理和缓存技术虽然能提高并行度并减少重复计算，但由于问题在不同prompt间被分离，注意力过程中的内存瓶颈导致GPU利用率不足，限制了可实现的加速效果。

Method: IPPD方法将多个共享上下文的问题合并到单个prompt中，使得每个问题下一个token的解码在一个推理步骤内完成，从而高效地共享注意力过程中的内存和计算资源。该方法通过虚拟位置ID和注意力掩码操作来保证输出与标准prompting一致，同时不需要微调或修改LLM架构。由于所有并行都发生在单个prompt内部，IPPD与批量推理完全兼容，即使每个prompt使用不同的上下文也适用。

Result: 实验表明，IPPD相比标准解码可提供高达7倍的有效吞吐量提升，且不产生质量下降；在大多数设置下，IPPD优于使用PagedAttention的prefix caching方法。

Conclusion: IPPD通过在单prompt内并行解码多个共享上下文的问答任务，有效解决了注意力过程中的内存瓶颈问题，实现了显著的吞吐量提升，且无需模型修改或微调，与现有批处理系统兼容。

Abstract: In common-context question answering (CCQA) tasks, multiple input questions share a common context to base their answers from. However, Large Language Models typically generate each answer using an independent prompt. While existing batching and caching techniques help improve parallelism and reduce repeated computations, the separation of questions across prompts limits the achievable speedup, as modern GPUs are underutilized due to a memory bottleneck during attention. We present Intra-Prompt Parallel Decoding (IPPD), a novel inference method that answers multiple common-context questions in parallel within a single prompt. IPPD directly addresses the bottleneck by efficiently sharing both memory and computation during the attention process, as the next token for every question is decoded in a single inference step. IPPD uses virtual position IDs and attention mask manipulation to generate the same output as standard prompting without requiring fine-tuning or any changes to the LLM architecture. Since all parallelism occurs within a prompt, IPPD is fully compatible with batched inference, even when each prompt features a different context. Our experiments show that IPPD delivers up to 7X the effective throughput as standard decoding without quality degradation, and outperforms prefix caching with PagedAttention in most settings.

</details>


### [64] [MedWER: A Reproducible, Model-Free Evaluation Protocol for Medical Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05728)
*Justin Behling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MedWER，一个用于医学自动语音识别（ASR）评估的协议与开源工具。针对传统词错误率（WER）忽略临床关键错误的问题，MedWER使用一个固定、许可证干净的医学术语列表（19,373个词条）作为分母，配合固定的文本归一化器实现短语感知的术语受限WER，并基于两个开放基准在Moonshine base、Whisper base.en和MedASR上报告了带95%置信区间的基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整体词错误率（WER）会掩盖临床上至关重要的错误——例如一段转录文本可以做到95%正确却仍将一种药物替换为另一种药物。现有的解决方案通常对医学实体进行加权，但几乎都依赖评估时的命名实体识别（NER）模型或云API，导致指标的分母变成一个版本化的黑盒，缺乏可复现性与可审计性。

Method: MedWER方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 分母为固定的、许可证干净（license-clean）的术语列表，包含19,373个从公开来源投射的药物、诊断、症状和伤害机制条目；(2) 协议绑定了一个固定的文本归一化器，与短语感知的术语受限WER（MedWER）结合，唯一需要版本化的组件是归一化器依赖，且其版本被精确锁定并通过提交的黄金（golden）测试夹具校验；(3) 覆盖率通过独立的省级药物受益文件（非构建该列表所用数据源）验证，匹配启发式则通过人工标注的真实实体跨度进行校准。

Result: 作者在Moonshine base、Whisper base.en和MedASR三个ASR模型上，基于两个开放基准基准测试集，使用发布的工具计算MedWER，并通过重采样的每个话语（per-utterance）分数报告95%置信区间。

Conclusion: MedWER提供了一种无黑盒、可版本化、可复现的医学ASR评估协议，其固定术语列表与非NER依赖的设计使得指标可审计、可比较，克服了传统WER在临床应用中掩盖关键错误的问题。

Abstract: Overall word error rate hides clinically critical errors: a transcript can be 95% correct and still swap one drug for another. The usual fix weights errors on medical entities, and almost always depends on an evaluation-time named-entity recognition (NER) model or cloud API, which makes the metric's denominator a versioned black box. We present MedWER, an evaluation protocol and open-source tool for medical ASR whose denominator is a fixed, license-clean term list: 19,373 drug, diagnosis, symptom, and injury-mechanism entries projected from public sources. The protocol couples a pinned text normalizer with a phrase-aware term-restricted WER, the MedWER, so the only versioned component is a normalizer dependency held at an exact release and checked against committed golden fixtures. Coverage is validated against an independent provincial drug-benefit file the list was not built from; the matching heuristic is calibrated against ground-truth entity spans. Baselines for Moonshine~base, Whisper~base.en, and MedASR on two open benchmarks are scored with the released tool and reported with 95% confidence intervals from resampled per-utterance scores.

</details>


### [65] [Some Tokens Behave like Magnets: Revealing Linguistic Organization in the Layers of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05743)
*Andrew Liu,Devan Srinivasan,Gerald Penn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大型语言模型（LLMs）中存在一类特殊的令牌向量，称为"磁向量"（magnetic vectors），它们通过吸引或排斥周围令牌来组织表征空间。研究发现功能词在浅层一致地表现为排斥磁向量，而深层磁向量会以独特方式重组极性。该现象跨架构、尺寸和层配置保持一致，且具有因果相关性：移除浅层排斥磁向量会使词性标注准确率从91%暴跌至10%以下，而移除深层吸引磁向量则破坏语义任务。这为理解LLM如何跨层几何化组织语言计算提供了首个无需探针的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大型语言模型内部如何组织和结构化解码语言是当前可解释性研究的核心挑战。以往方法多依赖探针（probing）来事后分析模型表征，缺乏对模型内部几何组织机制的直接洞察。本文旨在发现LLM内部一种自组织的几何驱动机制，从而打开一条无需探针即可理解语言模型跨层计算组织方式的路径。

Method: 本文通过系统分析LLMs中间层令牌向量的几何特性，识别出一类特别的向量（磁向量），它们像物理磁铁一样吸引或排斥周围令牌，影响邻近令牌向量的伸缩形态（同向吸引磁铁的令牌被拉长，同向排斥磁铁的令牌被压缩）。作者在多种LLM架构、尺寸和层配置上验证了这一现象的普遍性，并通过因果干预实验（移除特定层中的磁向量）以及微调下游任务观察磁向量分布变化来验证其因果相关性。

Result: （1）识别出"磁向量"这一特殊的令牌向量类别，具有吸引/排斥两种对立极性；（2）发现功能词在浅层一致作为排斥磁向量，且深层中磁向量以独特方式重组极性；（3）该模式在不同LLM架构、尺寸和层配置中保持一致；（4）因果实验表明浅层排斥磁向量对句法任务至关重要（移除后POS标注从91%降至<10%），深层吸引磁向量对语义任务关键；（5）微调后任务功能令牌涌现为磁向量，如问答任务中答案跨度令牌在最终层成为独特排斥磁向量，从几何上从上下文"雕刻"出答案。

Conclusion: LLMs内部存在一种类似物理磁场的向量组织机制，通过吸引与排斥两种极性在层间系统地组织语言计算。这一发现首次提供了无需探针即可理解LLM如何几何化组织语言处理过程的路径，建议后续研究进一步深入探索该机制。

Abstract: We identify a special group of token vectors inside large language models (LLMs), which we term magnetic vectors, that organize the surrounding tokens by either attracting or repelling them. Particularly, tokens pointing the same way as an attracting magnet are elongated; tokens pointing the same way as a repelling magnet are compressed. Just as physical magnets pull or push away the iron filings around them, these vectors organize their surroundings through two opposing polarities. Moreover, we identify a statistically significant pattern in linguistic category where function words consistently act as repelling magnets in early layers, and we also find magnets consistently reorganize their polarities in unique ways deeper in the model. In a further case study we find this observation may unveil a deliberate, layer-wise organization in how LLMs process language.
  This pattern is consistent across different LLM architectures, sizes, and layer configurations. It is also causally relevant.
