Abstract: Understanding whether language-model embeddings encode structured real-world information is important for both representation analysis and information retrieval. We study this question for temporal and geographic signals using a simple projection-based method that operates directly on output embeddings. Given a small set of seed examples, the method defines an axis in embedding space and ranks texts or entities by their projection onto that axis. Our approach is fully black-box and model-agnostic: it requires only embeddings, without access to model weights, internal activations, auxiliary probes, or additional training. This makes it applicable to modern embedding models available only through APIs and provides a lightweight way to analyze whether temporal and spatial dimensions are present in their representation spaces. We apply the method to temporal and geographic datasets and find that embedding projections recover meaningful chronological and spatial structure. These results provide evidence that output embeddings encode signals relevant to time and space, while also offering a practical tool for interpretability and for downstream temporal and geographic information retrieval tasks, such as temporal ordering, geographic ranking, and tagging.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond Prompts: Measuring and Optimizing LLM Tool-Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05736)
*Cen,Zhao,Haibo Ruan,Wenjie Chen,Pei-fen Tu,Usman Abbasi,Joel Hesch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究无需重训练的LLM工具智能体运行时（harness）优化，提出资源受限的固定模型多轮工具智能体harness选择框架。核心贡献是PRISM方法，通过聚类失败并路由修复到提示词或工具边界中间件等编辑面（在Pareto搜索内），在三个基准上取得显著性能提升，并提出了报告平均留出增益之外还需报告所选harness可靠性的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM工具智能体的性能可以通过修改运行时harness（提示词、工具接口、中间件、状态处理、恢复逻辑）而非重训练来提升，但如何系统化地在该搜索空间内选择最优harness缺乏统一且可靠的评估协议。现有优化器可能偶尔找到大幅提升却选择脆弱的更新，因此需要同时报告平均提升和可靠性指标。

Method: （1）将问题形式化为资源受限的固定模型多轮工具智能体harness选择；（2）搜索面限定为提示词与工具边界中间件，编辑被约束为工具边界的受控拦截而非任意重写；（3）提出优化器无关的评估协议，报告平均留出提升、最差条件提升、可重复性、日志成本诊断及RelLift95(B)保守估计；（4）实例化仅提示词与提示词加中间件两类优化器，其中PRISM通过聚类失败并将修复路由到提示词、工具边界中间件或联合编辑面，在Pareto搜索中进行选择。

Result: 在BFCL multi-round、tau2-Retail和tau2-Telecom上，PRISM分别获得14.2、14.9和10.1个百分点的平均留出提升，三个基准均获得正的实证RelLift95。组件消融表明性能优势主要来自失败表面路由和编辑模式约束。跨优化器结果显示，某些搜索过程偶尔能发现大幅提升但会选择脆弱的更新，因此需同时报告所选harness的可靠性与平均留出提升。

Conclusion: 无需重训练、仅通过优化运行时harness即可有效提升固定LLM工具智能体性能；PRISM的失败感知的编辑面路由与Pareto搜索策略是可靠且高效的方法；评估时应同时关注平均性能提升与所选方案在留出集上的可靠性（如RelLift95），而非仅看平均提升。

Abstract: LLM tool agents can be improved without retraining by modifying the runtime harness around a fixed model: prompts, tool interfaces, middleware, state handling, and recovery logic. We study this setting as resource-bounded harness selection for fixed-model multi-turn tool agents, with the search surface scoped to prompts and tool-boundary middleware: edits are guarded intercepts at the tool boundary, not arbitrary rewriting of agent execution logic. Our optimizer-agnostic protocol reports mean held-out lift, worst-condition lift, repeatability, logged cost diagnostics, and RelLift95(B), a conservative estimate of the held-out gain of the harness selected under budget B. We instantiate the protocol with prompt-only and prompt-plus-middleware optimizers, including PRISM, which clusters failures and routes repairs to prompt, tool-boundary middleware, or joint edit surfaces within a Pareto search. On BFCL multi-round, tau2-Retail, and tau2-Telecom, PRISM obtains mean held-out lifts of 14.2, 14.9, and 10.1 percentage points and positive empirical RelLift95 on all three benchmarks, and a component ablation attributes the margin chiefly to failure-surface routing and the edit-pattern constraint. Across optimizers, the results show that some search procedures can occasionally find large gains but still choose brittle updates, so the reliability of the chosen harness should be reported alongside average held-out lift.

</details>


### [134] [The Normalization of Deviance in AI Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05749)
*Emilio Barkett,Alexander Kimpton,Daniel Graham,Yusuf Kundgol*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，AI风险研究过度聚焦于能力风险（系统过于强大、自主或与人类价值观不一致），而忽视了开发这些系统的组织层面的结构性风险。通过分析挑战者号航天飞机、三里岛核事故和波音737 MAX空难三个历史案例，本文识别出每起灾难前共有的结构性机制，并将其映射到当代AI开发中，认为现有安全基础设施可能提供的保护比表面看起来更少——组织即使完全合规地完成安全流程，仍可能产生灾难性后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI风险研究主要集中在能力风险层面——系统自身是否过于强大、过于自主或与人类价值观错位——而对组织层面的风险关注甚少。本文试图填补这一空白，论证无论AI系统能力如何发展，构建这些系统的组织自身都面临着与过去重大技术灾难相同的结构性漂移倾向。

Method: 采用历史案例研究的比较分析方法，选取太空梭挑战者号事故、三里岛核事故和波音737 MAX坠机三起重大技术灾难作为案例，通过识别每起灾难前共同的结构性失败机制，将其归纳为普遍的组织性风险模式，再将这些模式逐一套用于当代AI开发组织的语境中进行映射分析。

Result: 研究发现，现有的AI安全基础设施可能提供的保护程度低于表面所示——组织可以在完全遵守安全流程的情况下仍产生灾难性结果。AI开发的前灾难期仍在进行中，意味着这些组织性风险模式目前仍然可以被干预和中断。

Conclusion: 本文的核心结论是：官僚化、合规化的安全流程可能制造"安全幻觉"，组织层面的结构性漂移是AI发展中的重要风险盲区。论文的目的是在灾难尚未发生之前，使这些动态机制变得可辨识、可干预，从而避免AI领域重蹈过去重大技术灾难的覆辙。

Abstract: Work on the risks of artificial intelligence has focused predominantly on capability risk: the danger that systems become too powerful, too autonomous, or too misaligned with human values. Far less attention has been paid to the organizational level---to whether the institutions building these systems are themselves predisposed to drift toward failure. This paper argues that they are. Regardless of how capable AI systems become, the organizations building them face the same structural dynamics that preceded past major technological disasters. Drawing on case studies of the Space Shuttle Challenger, the Three Mile Island accident, and the Boeing 737 MAX crashes, this paper identifies the common structural mechanisms preceding each failure and maps them onto contemporary AI development. The findings suggest that existing safety infrastructure may provide less protection than it appears, as organizations can complete safety processes in full compliance and still produce catastrophic outcomes. The pre-disaster period of AI development is still underway; the purpose of this paper is to make these dynamics legible while they can still be interrupted.

</details>


### [135] [From Monolithic Blending to Agentic Orchestration: Dynamic Response for Conversational Assistants at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05758)
*Cen,Zhao,Peng Wang,Chuan Shi,Yufeng Zhang,Ying Lyu,Wanmeng Ren,Robert Xue,Claire Na Cheng,Yashar Mehdad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文报告了在大型住宿市场（每月数百万对话、11种语言、P90延迟10秒）的客服助手生产系统中，将单一Qwen3-235B-A22B混合响应模型替换为带类型化工具的受限ReAct编排器加上较小生成器的架构迁移（称为Dynamic Response, DR）。通过重放同一轮对话隔离架构效果：实体选择精确率从8.3%提升至89.1%（召回从75.2%降至67.3%），结构化动作幻觉从2.14%降为0.0%；A/B测试显示硬升级响应从5.60%降至3.08%，软升级从9.56%降至2.49%；服务优化使编排器P90延迟从3.87秒降至2.24秒，GPU占用减少约三分之一，自托管使年模型服务成本降低超一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会话助手的架构设计面临一个关键选择：是将检索、动作选择、升级和措辞融合在单一模型路径中，还是将这些角色分离。作者希望在真实生产环境中验证架构分离的有效性，并区分架构效果与提示词、对齐、服务等混杂因素带来的影响，同时追求更低的延迟、成本和幻觉率。

Method: 将单一Qwen3-235B-A22B混合响应模型迁移为Dynamic Response架构：(1) 一个有界的ReAct编排器，基于类型化工具进行推理和行动选择；(2) 一个较小的生成模型，基于后端验证过的上下文契约来撰写回复。实体选择使用类型化实体选择模块，动作选择使用带成员检查的类型化动作ID。通过在不重复对话上重放实验隔离架构效果，并通过低流量A/B测试验证升级率变化。

Result: (1) 类型化实体选择将预订选择器的精确率从8.3%提升至89.1%，召回从75.2%略降至67.3%；(2) 类型化动作ID配合成员检查将结构化动作幻觉从2.14%降至0.0%；(3) A/B测试中硬升级从5.60%降至3.08%，软升级从9.56%降至2.49%，人工转接量基本持平，自助解决率方向性提升+5.1点（95% CI: [-2, +12]）；(4) 服务优化将编排器P90延迟从3.87秒降至2.24秒；(5) GPU占用减少约三分之一；(6) 自托管使年模型服务成本降低超一个数量级。

Conclusion: 在生产规模下，将会话助手从单一大型模型混合响应器分离为"类型化工具编排+较小生成器"的模块化架构是可行且有效的：能显著提升精确率并消除结构化幻觉，在保证人工转接量基本不变的前提下降低自动升级率，同时大幅降低延迟和推理成本。论文强调严格控制混杂因素、仅用相同重放轮次归因架构效果的方法论价值。

Abstract: Conversational assistants can blend retrieval, action selection, escalation, and wording in a single model path, or separate those roles. We report a production migration of a customer-support assistant at a large accommodation marketplace (millions of conversations per month, 11 languages, 10-second P90). Dynamic Response (DR) replaces a single Qwen3-235B-A22B blended responder with a bounded ReAct orchestrator over typed tools plus a smaller generator that writes from a backend-validated context contract. Because the migration also changed prompts, alignment, and serving, we attribute each effect to its cause and claim as architecture effects only those measured on identical replayed turns: typed entity selection moves the reservation selector to a precision-first operating point (precision 8.3% to 89.1%, recall 75.2% to 67.3%), and typed action IDs with a membership check remove observed structured-action hallucination (2.14% to 0.0%). A low-ramp A/B test reproduces the replay escalation reductions: hard-escalation responses fall from 5.60% to 3.08% and soft-escalation responses from 9.56% to 2.49%, while production handoff volume holds roughly steady; self-solve is directional (+5.1 points, 95% CI [-2, +12]). Serving optimizations cut orchestrator P90 latency from 3.87s to 2.24s on a GPU footprint reduced by roughly one-third, and self-hosting reduces estimated annual model-serving cost by more than an order of magnitude.

</details>


### [136] [Inference-Time Graph Engineering for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05774)
*Katherine Tieu,Dongqi Fu,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Hong Yan,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReActNet，一个无需训练的框架，从图工程角度将多智能体LLM编排重新定义为编译任务条件的时间工作流图，每个图快照对应一个推理阶段，每条边携带自然语言指令描述通信语义。该框架将图编译与执行分离，在知识推理、数学问题求解、代码生成和GAIA助手任务上均优于固定拓扑和已学习拓扑的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多智能体LLM的系统高度依赖图结构化通信来协调专门化的智能体，但大多数方法仅优化静态拓扑结构，缺乏对动态、任务条件化的编排以及智能体间边级通信语义的显式建模，从而限制多智能体协调的适应性和可解释性。

Method: ReActNet将查询和一组角色专门化的智能体编译成一组连续的有向通信图。每个图快照对应一个推理阶段，每条边携带自然语言指令，指明源智能体应向目标智能体提供的消息内容。复合的时间图通过结构化消息传递执行：智能体整合先前状态与控制器指定邻居发来的消息来更新其推理状态，最终由聚合器将各状态综合为答案。该方法明确分离了图编译与图执行，无需强化学习或基于梯度的拓扑优化。

Result: 在知识推理、数学问题求解、代码生成和GAIA助手任务等基准上，ReActNet一致优于固定拓扑和已学习拓扑的基线方法，同时保持具有竞争力的推理成本。

Conclusion: 有效的多智能体编排不仅取决于哪些智能体进行通信，还取决于如何工程设计可执行的工作流图，该图应编码推理过程中信息流动的时间（when）、原因（why）和方式（how）。

Abstract: Recent multi-agent LLM systems increasingly rely on graph-structured communication to coordinate specialized agents. We revisit multi-agent orchestration from a graph-engineering perspective: rather than optimizing a static topology, we synthesize a task-conditioned temporal workflow graph that jointly specifies agent connectivity and edge-level communication semantics. We introduce ReActNet, a training-free framework that compiles a query and a set of role-specialized agents into a sequence of directed communication graphs. Each graph snapshot corresponds to one reasoning stage, and each edge carries a natural-language instruction specifying the message that a source agent should provide to a target agent. The compiled temporal graph is then executed through structured message passing: agents update their reasoning states by integrating their previous states with messages from controller-assigned neighbors, and a final aggregator synthesizes the resulting states into the answer. This design separates graph compilation from graph execution, making multi-agent coordination explicit, inspectable, and task-conditioned without requiring reinforcement learning or gradient-based topology optimization. Across knowledge reasoning, mathematical problem solving, code generation, and GAIA-style assistant tasks, ReActNet consistently improves over fixed-topology and learned-topology baselines while maintaining competitive inference cost. These results suggest that effective multi-agent orchestration depends not only on which agents communicate, but also on engineering executable workflow graphs that encode when, why, and how information should flow during reasoning.

</details>


### [137] [DI-Bench: Systematically Generating In-Domain Data Intelligence Benchmarks for Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05776)
*Jiangyun Zhang,Kristen Surrao,Torpong Nitayanont,Yupei Zhang,Roopali Singh,Zhiyu Chen,Julia Huang,Zhou Tang,Shayan Ali Akbar,Omar Alonso,Erwin Cornejo,Yuan Li,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DI-Bench是一个自动化生成数据智能(BI)领域基准评测数据的流水线，通过构建工件关联图将数据表、维度、指标与文档关联，生成需结合结构化数据计算与业务知识检索的评测任务。在两个公开数据集上生成731个任务，评测发现四个模型在需要业务规则修改计算的任务上准确率仅32%，揭示了现有模型在企业数据智能场景中的显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级智能代理需要在特定领域基准上进行评测，但现有公开基准很少评测代理能否将业务知识与分析计算相结合，且人工构建此类基准成本高昂。

Method: DI-Bench流水线首先构建数据表、维度、指标和文档之间的工件关联图，形成同时涉及结构化数据和关联知识的问题；通过查询执行得到金标准答案，再使用LLM进行问题生成与验证。流水线应用于两个公开数据集，产出731个任务的基准。

Result: 生成731个任务覆盖知识检索、分析计算和规则接地推理三类。评测四个模型后发现一个关键结果：在检索到的业务规则会修改计算的场景下，模型准确率仅32%，表明当前模型在需要计算与知识整合的任务上表现显著不足。

Conclusion: DI-Bench提供了一种低成本、可扩展的企业级数据智能基准构建方案，同时揭示了现有模型在结合业务知识与分析计算方面的明显短板，为后续模型能力的改进提供了针对性的评测方向。

Abstract: Evaluating enterprise agents on domain-specific benchmarks is critical, yet public benchmarks rarely evaluate whether agents can integrate business knowledge with analytical computation, and constructing such benchmarks manually is costly. We present DI-Bench, a pipeline for generating realistic benchmarks for data intelligence (DI), the practice of extracting insights from large volumes of enterprise data. To emulate realistic DI tasks that require both computation and knowledge retrieval, DI-Bench builds an artifact linkage graph over data tables, dimensions, metrics, and documents to form questions involving structured data and associated knowledge. Ground truth answers are derived via query execution, followed by LLM question generation and validation. Applied to two public datasets, the pipeline produces a 731-task benchmark covering knowledge retrieval, analytical computation, and rule-grounded reasoning. To show the discriminatory capability and difficulty of the benchmark, we evaluate four models, revealing a substantial finding: models achieve only 32% accuracy when doing computational tasks where retrieved business rules modify the computation.

</details>


### [138] [Distilling Vision-Language Models for On-Device Fire Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05782)
*Mohammad Kazzazi,Zixuan Liu,Siavash Khajavi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将领域专用的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）通过教师-学生知识蒸馏框架压缩为轻量级学生模型，实现在商业火灾检测传感器上的完全端侧部署。实验表明压缩后的紧凑模型保留了教师模型大部分火灾理解能力，且部署不仅影响推理精度还影响模型失败模式，其中Qwen2.5-0.5B在推理精度、延迟和内存使用之间提供了最优的综合权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统火灾检测系统因缺乏语义上下文推理能力容易产生误报，视觉-语言模型（VLMs）通过理解场景语义可减少误报，但其体量过大，难以部署在资源受限的嵌入式火灾传感器上。因此，如何在保证安全关键行为不丢失的前提下压缩领域专用VLM进行端侧部署，是本文的核心研究动机。

Method: 提出一个教师-学生知识蒸馏框架，将针对火灾理解进行微调的大规模VLM作为教师，通过知识蒸馏将火灾理解能力迁移到轻量级学生模型中。研究方法涵盖多个VLM家族和不同模型尺度进行比较实验，并将蒸馏后的模型部署到商业产品Detectium火灾检测传感器上进行联合评估。

Result: 在多个VLM家族和模型尺度上的实验表明，紧凑的学生模型能够保留教师模型的大部分火灾理解能力。在Detectium火灾传感器上的实际部署评估（同时考察推理精度、延迟和内存）显示，模型压缩和部署不仅影响推理精度，还显著改变模型的失败模式。Qwen2.5-0.5B在整体部署权衡上表现最优。

Conclusion: 本研究为在资源受限且安全关键的场景中部署领域专用VLM提供了系统性的指导。关键发现是部署优化不仅要关注精度指标，还需考虑压缩对模型失败模式的影响。Qwen2.5-0.5B作为最优学生模型的选择，为嵌入式火灾检测中的VLM部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) offer a promising alternative to conventional fire detection systems by reasoning about the semantic context of a scene and thus reducing false alarms, yet their large model size makes deployment on embedded fire sensors impractical. In this paper, we study how domain-specialized VLMs can be compressed for fully on-device deployment without losing the safety-critical behavior required for fire detection. We develop a teacher-student knowledge distillation framework in which large VLMs fine-tuned for fire understanding can be distilled into lightweight students. Experiments across multiple VLM families and model scales show that compact students preserve most of their teachers' fire-understanding capability. We further deploy the distilled models on our commercial Detectium fire detection sensor and jointly evaluate reasoning accuracy, latency, and memory usage. The results show that compression and deployment affect not only accuracy but also model failure modes, with Qwen2.5-0.5B providing the strongest overall deployment trade-off. Our findings provide broader guidance for deploying domain-specialized VLMs in resource-constrained, safety-critical settings.

</details>


### [139] [More Than Mimicking Reviewers: Evaluating LLMs for Pre-Submission Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05788)
*Pouya Parsa,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究面向作者的LLM预审系统：在大规模ICLR 2026投稿上，系统先产生大量原子关注点再压缩为短报告。独立采样覆盖44.9%历史问题；去重与回填后严格覆盖达78.7%、严重性加权覆盖84.9%（代价为3.6倍请求和5.2倍token）。隐藏的Top-32 Oracle可保留256候选池的79.3%加权覆盖，但仅用论文文本选择时覆盖率降到40-44%。结论认为LLM评审覆盖面广但压缩能力弱，归因于代表性选择和匹配器灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行评审反馈通常来得太晚，作者无法在投稿前做出有意义的修改。本文旨在将部分"压力测试"前置到投稿环节，通过面向作者的LLM系统在投稿前生成评审意见，帮助作者提前发现并修正论文潜在问题。

Method: 构建一个LLM评审系统：首先生成一大池子原子关注点（atomic concerns），再将它们压缩成一份简短报告。从10,000篇ICLR 2026投稿中选取3,398篇有投稿前可获取版本的稿件，与历史评审进行一致性评估，并单独考察被遗漏关注点的潜在有效性。采用十篇论文诊断集，对比独立采样、去重+回填等压缩策略，并引入隐藏Top-32 Oracle与论文-only选择器进行覆盖率对比。

Result: 独立采样覆盖44.9%的历史问题；去重和回填使严格覆盖率提升至78.7%、严重性加权覆盖率84.9%，但请求量增加3.6倍、token消耗增加5.2倍。隐藏Top-32 Oracle可保留256候选池中79.3%的加权覆盖率，但仅基于论文文本的选择器仅保留40-44%的覆盖率。消融实验表明代表性选择和匹配器灵敏度是压缩效果不佳的主要原因。

Conclusion: LLM评审在拥有大规模候选池时覆盖面很广，但压缩效果不佳。覆盖率损失的主要来源是候选选择的代表性问题以及匹配器（matcher）的灵敏度不足，提示未来改进应聚焦于更好的候选选择策略和更鲁棒的评分匹配机制。

Abstract: Peer-review feedback often arrives too late for authors to make meaningful revisions. We study an author-facing LLM system that moves part of this stress test before submission: it generates a broad pool of atomic concerns and compresses them into a short report. We evaluate agreement with historical reviews and, separately, the possible validity of concerns they omit.
  From 10,000 ICLR 2026 submissions, we use 3,398 manuscripts with accessible versions that predate review. On a ten-paper diagnostic, independent sampling covers 44.9% of historical issues; deduplication and refill reaches 78.7% strict and 84.9% seriousness-weighted coverage, at 3.6$\times$ more requests and 5.2$\times$ more tokens. A hidden Top-32 Oracle preserves the full 79.3% weighted coverage of a 256-candidate pool, but paper-only selectors retain only 40--44%. LLM review therefore provides broad coverage with a large candidate pool but compresses poorly; ablations identify representative selection and matcher sensitivity as the main sources of this gap.

</details>


### [140] [Spillover-Aware Multi-Value Steering for Pluralistic LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05800)
*Weici Pan,Xander Barron,Jiawei Zhou,Zhenhua Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多维度激活定向（multi-dimensional activation steering）方法，解决现有定向方法一次只能处理单一概念的问题。研究发现朴素定向会产生显著的溢出效应（spillover），即针对某一价值观的干预会泄漏到其他维度。作者将其类比为因果推断中的处理效应与溢出效应分解，将问题归因于定向方向间的几何纠缠（由Gram矩阵刻画），并从激活范数惩罚目标中推导出零成本校正，实现各方向贡献的精确解耦。整个流程无需微调、奖励模型或人工提示工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活定向（activation steering）方法只能处理单一概念，而多元对齐（pluralistic alignment）需要不同利益相关者对多种价值观有不同侧重，因此需要同时调控多个维度。然而朴素的多维定向会产生严重溢出效应，使得特定价值观的干预效果泄漏到其他维度。

Method: 将定向效应分解为处理效应与溢出效应（类比因果推断）；通过定向方向间的Gram矩阵刻画几何纠缠；从激活范数惩罚目标推导出零成本校正公式对方向贡献进行解耦；构建端到端管道，仅需领域问题即可自动发现价值观维度、提取定向方向、诊断纠缠并施加校正后的定向。

Result: 在气候话语（climate discourse）任务上，校正将净定向效应从+5.9%提升至+14.0%，该结果由超过100,000次成对判断验证。

Conclusion: 通过精确解耦多个激活定向方向的贡献，可以实现高效、零成本的多维价值对齐调控，弥补了现有激活定向方法在多概念控制上的不足，为多元对齐提供了可行且可扩展的技术路径。

Abstract: Activation steering controls LLM behavior at inference time by adding learned directions to hidden states, but existing methods handle one concept at a time. Pluralistic alignment, where different stakeholders need different value emphases, requires steering multiple dimensions simultaneously. We show that naive steering produces substantial spillover: the effect intended for one value leaks into others. This parallels the treatment-versus-spillover decomposition in causal inference. We trace spillover to geometric entanglement of steering directions, captured by their Gram matrix, and derive a zero-cost correction from an activation-norm-penalized objective that decouples each direction's contribution exactly. Our end-to-end pipeline requires no fine-tuning, no reward model, and no manual prompt engineering: given only domain questions, it automatically discovers value dimensions, extracts directions, diagnoses entanglement, and applies corrected steering. On climate discourse, the correction improves the net steering effect from +5.9% to +14.0%, validated over 100,000 pairwise judgments.

</details>


### [141] [Evidence-Aligned Local Composition of Discrete Experts for Sequence Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05801)
*Mohammad Panahazari,Usman A. Khan,Shuchin Aeron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出证据对齐的局部组合（evidence-aligned local composition）方法，在无区域标签或无训练路由器的条件下，通过从损坏观察的边际证据中推断对专家（域模型）的逐位置软加权，实现对由多域文本混合而成的损坏文档的恢复。权重由专家自身的去噪损失估计证据并跨位置平滑，具备软组合特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档可视为来自不同领域的文本组合（如README包含散文、代码和配置）。当文档被损坏且仅能使用冻结的领域专家模型时，恢复文档需要同时决定缺失内容以及每个位置应该信任哪个专家，且这是在测试时进行的，没有区域标签或已训练的路由器。现有方法无法在测试时动态确定每个位置的专家权重。

Method: 提出证据对齐的局部组合方法，其核心步骤为：(1) 从给定损坏模型下损坏观察的边际证据中推断专家集合上软逐位置权重；(2) 通过专家自身的去噪损失估计证据；(3) 跨位置平滑权重。权重是软性的，因此在真实组合是混合的时恢复混合，在单个专家足以胜任时集中于该专家。

Result: 在分类模拟器、字节级专家和从1.3B离散流匹配模型微调的专家上进行评估。在自然混合的科学文档上达到0.85的区域字段准确率，在区域词汇不相交的构造混合物上达到0.98。当专家真正不同时，恢复效果优于单一全局权重；当专家收敛时退化为全局权重，并能跟踪专家分离度的度量。

Conclusion: 实验表明该方法能有效在测试时推断逐位置的专家权重，从而在无监督条件下恢复多域混合的损坏文档，其性能取决于专家之间的真正差异程度。

Abstract: A document modeled as a discrete sequence of tokens can be thought of as being generated from a composition of texts from different domains; a README file, for example, moves between prose, code, and configuration. When such a document is corrupted and only frozen domain experts are available, restoring it requires deciding both what is missing and which expert to trust at each position, at test time and without region labels or a trained router. We introduce evidence-aligned local composition, which infers a soft, position-wise weighting over the experts from the marginal evidence of the corrupted observation under a given corruption model, estimating the evidence from the experts' own denoising losses and smoothing the weights across positions. Because the weighting is soft, it recovers a mixture when the true composition is mixed and concentrates on one expert when that suffices. Across a categorical simulator, byte-level experts, and experts fine-tuned from a $1.3$B discrete flow-matching model, the inferred weights track the true regions at $0.85$ field accuracy on naturally mixed scientific documents, and at $0.98$ on constructed mixtures whose regions are lexically disjoint. Restoration improves over a single global weight when the experts are genuinely distinct and reduces to it when they converge, tracking a measure of expert separation.

</details>


### [142] [Exposing Weaknesses in Emotion Recognition in Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05806)
*Amir Ben Khalifa,Fanny Bezancon,Amine Trabelsi,Bessam Abdulrazak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文质疑了对话情感识别（ERC）中"每句话可被赋予单一情感标签"的核心假设，通过在零样本设置下使用大语言模型（LLMs）研究发现：聚合指标掩盖了系统性问题，错误集中在包含否定、感叹和插入语的语句中。人工再标注研究显示只有35%的案例存在强一致性，表明许多"模型错误"实为真实标注歧义。为此提出了LLM-as-Judge评估框架，对每个情感独立评估其在对话语境中的合理性，而非强制单标签决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ERC方法多依赖任务特定微调，可能利用数据集特有线索；同时研究中普遍默认每个语句具有单一明确的情感标签，这一假设从未被认真检验。作者旨在通过零样本设置下的LLM研究来审视这一基本假设是否成立。

Method: 在零样本设置下使用LLMs进行ERC研究，结合前序对话轮次作为上下文；通过细粒度错误分析识别错误集中模式；进行四名人工标注者的受控再标注研究评估标注一致性；提出LLM-as-Judge框架，将每个情感类别作为独立评估单元，依据其在对话语境中的合理性而非强制单标签选择进行评估。

Result: 错误集中在包含否定、感叹和插入语的语句中，且此模式在所有评估模型中一致出现，暗示是基准（benchmark）本身的局限而非模型缺陷；人工再标注显示仅35%的案例存在强一致标注，中性语句主导高一致性区域，而许多情感类别落在低一致性区域。

Conclusion: 许多看似模型的错误实际上反映了真实存在的标注歧义，而非模型情感理解能力不足。标准的单标签评估方式不足以准确衡量ERC模型的性能，需要转向更细粒度、容忍歧义的多标签式评估方法。

Abstract: Emotion Recognition in Conversations (ERC) aims to identify speakers' emotions in multi-turn dialogue. Accurate emotion recognition can support a wide range of applications, including empathetic conversational agents, mental health support, and educational technologies. While many recent approaches rely on task-specific fine-tuning, such models may exploit dataset-specific cues. A central yet rarely questioned assumption in ERC is that each utterance can be assigned a single unambiguous emotion label. To investigate this assumption, we study ERC using Large Language Models (LLMs) in a zero-shot setting while incorporating preceding conversational turns as context. We show that aggregate metrics mask systematic failures. Errors concentrate around utterances containing negations, exclamations, and interjections. This pattern is consistent across all evaluated models, suggesting limitations in the benchmarks rather than model-specific weaknesses. A controlled re-annotation study involving four human annotators supports this finding: strong agreement is observed in only 35 percent of cases, with neutral utterances dominating high-agreement instances, while many emotional categories fall into low-agreement regimes. These findings suggest that many apparent model errors reflect genuine annotation ambiguity rather than poor emotion understanding. Standard single-label evaluation is therefore insufficient. To address this limitation, we introduce an LLM-as-Judge framework that evaluates each emotion independently according to its plausibility in the conversational context rather than enforcing a single-label decision.

</details>


### [143] [Agentic BAIM-LLM Evaluation (ABLE): Benchmarking LLM Use of Protein Design Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05818)
*Bryce Cai,Geetha Jeyapragasan,Samira Nedungadi,Jake Yukich,Seth Donoughe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入ABLE基准，用于评估LLM代理在双重用途蛋白质设计工作流中使用生物AI模型（如ProteinMPNN和AlphaFold3）的能力。通过对15个前沿模型进行评估，发现7个模型拒绝所有任务，其余模型性能差异显著，其中Claude Sonnet 4和Gemini 3 Pro在信息检索、工具选择和工具使用方面得分最高。结果显示当前LLM能显著降低蛋白质设计门槛，但在规划、策略生成及生物知识与工具使用整合方面仍不够稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质设计涉及双重用途风险（既可用于有益开发也可能被滥用），随着LLM代理能够调用生物AI工具，需要系统评估其在蛋白质设计工作流中的能力，以理解潜在风险并指导安全治理。

Method: 构建ABLE基准，包含涵盖结构检索、序列生成和设计验证的任务集；对15个前沿LLM模型进行评估，比较其BIAM调用能力；在部分任务上与专家人类基线进行对比。

Result: 15个模型中7个拒绝执行所有任务；Claude Sonnet 4和Gemini 3 Pro表现最佳，在信息检索、工具选择和工具使用维度取得最高分；模型与专家人类表现存在差距，总体在规划、策略生成和知识与工具整合方面不一致。

Conclusion: 当前LLM确实能大幅降低蛋白质设计的准入门槛，但其能力尚不稳定，尤其是在高阶规划、策略制定以及将生物学知识与工具应用深度结合方面，提示需要在能力评估与安全防护之间寻求平衡。

Abstract: We introduce ABLE, a benchmark for evaluating LLM agents' ability to use biological AI models (BAIMs), such as ProteinMPNN and AlphaFold3, in dual-use protein design workflows. ABLE assesses agent performance through a set of tasks spanning structure retrieval, sequence generation, and design validation. We evaluate 15 frontier models and find that seven refuse all tasks, while the remaining models exhibit substantial performance differences. Claude Sonnet 4 and Gemini 3 Pro achieve the highest scores across information retrieval, tool selection, and tool use. We further compare model performance on a subset of tasks against an expert human baseline. Our results suggest that current LLMs can substantially lower barriers to protein design, but remain inconsistent in planning, strategy generation, and integrating biological knowledge with tool use.

</details>


### [144] [Beyond Top-$k$ Skill Retrieval: Diversity-Aware Skill Routing for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05824)
*Wang Wei,Tiankai Yang,Samyadeep Basu,Hongjie Chen,Yue Zhao,Zhengzhong Tu,Xiyang Hu,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan A. Rossi,Hoda Eldardiry*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Diverse Skill Routing (DSR)，一种基于行列式点过程（DPP）的多样性感知重排序框架，用于在大型技能库中路由LLM代理的用户请求，通过平衡相关性与非冗余性来改善多技能查询的技能选择效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理依赖外部技能时，在大型技能注册表上进行用户请求路由存在困难：许多技能功能冗余，而复杂任务往往需要互补的技能组合。现有技能路由器通常独立按查询相关性对候选技能排序，可能在冗余技能上浪费上下文预算。

Method: DSR采用行列式点过程（DPP）作为多样性感知的重排序框架，平衡相关性与非冗余性。其核心创新是引入查询残差多样性核（query-residual diversity kernel），惩罚冗余的技能重叠，同时减少仅因共享查询相关性引起的过度惩罚。

Result: 在SkillRouter基准上，DSR相对于强点式重排序基线在召回率和完整覆盖率上均有提升，且在多变技能查询上增益更大。

Conclusion: 技能路由不应仅被视为相关性排序问题，还应作为互补技能集合选择问题来处理。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external skills, but routing user requests over large skill registries is difficult because many skills are functionally redundant while complex tasks often require complementary skill sets. Existing skill routers typically rank candidates independently by query relevance, which can waste context budget on redundant skills. We propose Diverse Skill Routing (DSR), a diversity-aware reranking framework that uses a Determinantal Point Process to balance relevance and non-redundancy. DSR introduces a query-residual diversity kernel that penalizes redundant skill overlap while reducing penalties caused only by shared query relevance. On the SkillRouter benchmark, DSR improves recall and full coverage over a strong pointwise reranking baseline, with larger gains on multi-skill queries. These results suggest that skill routing should be treated not only as relevance ranking, but also as complementary set selection.

</details>


### [145] [Learning Counterfactual World Models for Embodied Reasoning under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05834)
*Todd Y. Zhou,Daniel Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"反事实坍缩"（counterfactual collapse）这一失败模式：世界模型虽能预测视觉上合理的未来，却无法区分产生不同行为后果的干预。为此引入反事实潜在世界模型（CLWM），通过对比反事实目标分离不同干预诱导的未来状态，在多个任务上提升规划成功率，并提出与规划成功率高度相关的反事实可分性指标用于审计编码器的可行动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型的表征越来越多地从大规模视频、交互和多模态语料中预训练而来，但预测质量无法回答一个关键问题：何时学到的表征真正可行动（actionable）？仅优化感知相似性的预训练编码器可能产生"反事实坍缩"——做出视觉上可信的预测却无法区分行为后果不同的干预，从而无法支撑规划与决策。

Method: 提出反事实潜在世界模型（CLWM），包含三部分：(1) 循环信念状态编码器（recurrent belief-state encoder）；(2) 动作条件潜在动力学（action-conditioned latent dynamics）；(3) 对比反事实目标（contrastive counterfactual objective），用于在观测外观相似时仍分离不同干预诱导的未来状态。此外提出表示无关（representation-agnostic）的反事实可分性指标（counterfactual separability metric）来审计任意编码器的可行动性。

Result: 在遮挡操作（Occluded Push）、别名导航（Aliased Maze）和长时程操作（Deferred Kitchen）任务上，CLWM较最强基线提升了规划成功率（Occluded Push：65.1%→74.6%；Aliased Maze：67.3%→78.9%），并将剥削式规划失败率从18.4%降至9.7%（Deferred Kitchen）。消融实验表明增益主要来自硬反事实负样本，尤其是感知别名负样本（perceptual-alias negatives）。反事实可分性指标在五种基线模型类别上与规划成功率相关性r≥0.94。

Conclusion: 优化感知相似性而非干预结构的表征必然导致反事实坍缩，使其不可行动。CLWM通过对比反事实目标有效解决此问题，反事实可分性指标可无需依赖具体表示结构、仅凭干预-结果标签审计任何编码器（预训练或从零训练）。本文尚未在大规模预训练编码器上测量该指标，目前仅建立指标本身及其与从零训练世界模型规划成功率的关系。

Abstract: World models promise a general route to embodied intelligence: learn predictive dynamics once, then reason, plan, and act with them. Increasingly, the representations beneath such models are pretrained on large-scale video, interaction, and multimodal corpora, which raises a question prediction quality alone cannot answer: when is a learned representation actually actionable? We identify a failure mode we call counterfactual collapse: a model predicts visually plausible futures while failing to distinguish interventions with different behavioral consequences. This arises whenever a representation is optimized for perceptual similarity rather than intervention structure, which is precisely the objective under which most large-scale pretrained encoders are learned. We introduce Counterfactual Latent World Models (CLWM), which combine a recurrent belief-state encoder, action-conditioned latent dynamics, and a contrastive counterfactual objective that separates futures induced by distinct interventions even when their observations look alike. Across occluded manipulation, aliased navigation, and long-horizon manipulation, CLWM improves planning success over the strongest baseline (65.1% $\to$ 74.6% on Occluded Push and 67.3% $\to$ 78.9% on Aliased Maze) and reduces exploitative planning failures (18.4% $\to$ 9.7% on Deferred Kitchen), with ablations attributing the gains to hard counterfactual negatives, especially perceptual-alias negatives. Finally, our counterfactual separability metric, which tracks planning success across the five baseline model classes ($r \ge 0.94$), is representation-agnostic: given intervention-outcome labels, it can audit any encoder, pretrained or trained from scratch, before a planner trusts it. We do not yet measure it on large-scale pretrained encoders. Here we establish the metric and its relationship to planning success for world models trained from scratch.

</details>


### [146] [AgentBrew: Offline Tool-Use Agent Learning from Raw Real-World Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05837)
*Zhiyi Lyu,Yewen Li,Longtao Zheng,Shengtian Yang,Lang Feng,Lei Feng,Peng Jiang,Kun Gai,Qingpeng Cai,Bo An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentBrew是一个面向LLM智能体的离线训练框架，无需任务验证器或在线rollout，仅从单批原始交互轨迹中学习有效的工具使用策略。通过"回顾性任务推断"从每条轨迹的实际结果重建对齐指令，并通过基于PMI的信用分配将轨迹信息分解为逐动作信用，用于加权策略训练目标。在GitHub、Notion、PostgreSQL三个真实MCP应用上，AgentBrew使Qwen3-32B平均提升+8.7 Acc/+9.7 Score，超越Qwen3-235B，并优于拒绝采样方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过工具API在真实应用中的部署日益增多，但在特定环境中训练它们仍然非常困难：真实应用没有预定义任务或验证器，没有忠实的模拟器，可用于大规模环境交互的预算也有限。现有的训练方法（如有监督微调、拒绝采样、在线强化学习等）往往依赖任务定义、奖励信号或交互式rollout，在真实环境约束下难以直接应用。因此需要一个能够仅从原始离线轨迹中提取训练信号的框架。

Method: AgentBrew包含三个核心步骤：(1) 环境探索阶段，收集未经质量过滤的原始轨迹语料；(2) 回顾性任务推断（Retrospective Task Inference），基于每条轨迹的实际结果重建与该轨迹对齐的指令；(3) 基于PMI的信用分配（PMI-Based Credit Assignment），通过点互信息将轨迹关于推断指令的总信息分解为可加性的逐动作信用。这些信用用于加权策略训练目标，放大信息量大的动作同时抑制无效动作。整个过程无需任务验证器或迭代的on-policy rollout。

Result: 在三个真实MCP应用（GitHub、Notion、PostgreSQL）上，AgentBrew使Qwen3-32B平均提升+8.7 Accuracy/+9.7 Score，超越了更大的Qwen3-235B（+2.3/+4.4），并优于拒绝采样（+5.9/+10.3）。结果表明，细粒度的离线学习能够从原始轨迹中恢复基于过滤方法会丢弃的有用监督信号。

Conclusion: AgentBrew证明了在没有任务验证器、模拟器和在线rollout的情况下，仅利用单批离线原始轨迹即可有效训练工具使用智能体。细粒度的信用分配（PMI-based）比粗粒度的过滤策略（如拒绝采样）能更充分地利用噪声轨迹中的监督信号。该框架为真实环境中LLM智能体的离线训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed in real-world applications through tool-use APIs, yet training them for specific environments remains fundamentally difficult: real-world applications provide no pre-defined tasks or verifiers, no faithful simulators, and limited budget for large-scale environment interaction. In this paper, we propose \textbf{AgentBrew}, an offline training framework that learns effective tool-use policies from a single batch of raw interaction trajectories, without task verifiers or iterative on-policy rollouts. The agent first explores the target environment to collect a raw trajectory corpus without quality filtering. To extract training signal from this noisy corpus, \emph{retrospective task inference} reconstructs an aligned instruction for each trajectory based on its actual outcome, and \emph{PMI-Based credit assignment} decomposes the trajectory's total information about the inferred instruction into additive per-action credits via pointwise mutual information (PMI). These credits weight the policy training objective, amplifying informative actions while suppressing ineffective ones. On three real-world MCP applications (GitHub, Notion, PostgreSQL), AgentBrew improves Qwen3-32B by +8.7 Acc / +9.7 Score on average, surpassing Qwen3-235B (+2.3 / +4.4) and outperforming rejection sampling (+5.9 / +10.3). These results demonstrate that fine-grained offline learning can recover useful supervision from raw trajectories that filtering-based approaches would discard. The code is available at https://github.com/alphatogo/AgentBrew

</details>


### [147] [Multimodal Resource-Exhaustion Attacks on Vision-Language Models via Joint Pixel-Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05889)
*Zhaoxiong Ni,Yatie Xiao,Chi-Man Pun,Fei Peng,Qingxiao Guan,Keke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出联合像素-提示优化（JPPO）框架，首次将可见提示与图像扰动同时作为对抗优化的联合变量，针对自回归视觉语言模型（VLM）实施跨模态资源耗尽攻击，在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上实现4.6倍延迟和5.3倍能耗放大，在BLIP-2上实现36.6倍延迟和32.7倍能耗放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对自回归视觉语言模型的资源耗尽攻击主要遵循单模态威胁模型，将图像分支作为唯一优化面而保持用户可见提示固定，即使最近的循环中心变体也局限于这种单通道范式。这使得资源可用性攻击从未被探索为跨模态联合优化问题，忽视了同时对多个可控输入面进行协同优化的攻击潜力。

Method: JPPO采用受限联合输入威胁模型，通过耦合的分阶段优化同时调整像素表面和提示表面。该方法在像素层面施加8/255无穷范数预算约束，通过联合优化的方式让两种模态的攻击效果产生协同放大效应，从机制上区别于依赖循环的失效模式。

Result: 在MS COCO和ImageNet数据集上评估五个开源VLM家族，JPPO在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上实现4.6倍延迟放大和5.3倍能耗放大，在BLIP-2上实现36.6倍延迟放大和32.7倍能耗放大，超越所有直接对比的基线，且需要更少的优化迭代次数。消融实验证实放大效应来源于多模态协调而非简单的提示长度或单一模态因素。

Conclusion: JPPO揭示了当前VLM部署防御中的结构性盲区：既有防御大多针对单一模态攻击设计，无法应对跨模态协同优化的资源耗尽攻击。研究结果呼吁将成本感知鲁棒性评估作为多模态部署的一等安全需求加以对待。

Abstract: Resource-exhaustion attacks against autoregressive vision-language models (VLMs) typically assume unimodal threat models, treating the image branch as the primary optimization surface while holding user-visible prompts fixed. Even recent loop-centric variants remain confined to this single-channel paradigm, leaving the exploitation of availability unexplored as a cross-modal optimization problem over jointly controllable input surfaces.
  We introduce Joint Pixel-Prompt Optimization (JPPO), the first compound adversarial framework elevating the visible prompt to a first-class adversarial variable alongside image perturbations. Under a restricted joint-input threat model, JPPO performs coupled, stagewise optimization over both the pixel and prompt surfaces. This produces synergistic cost amplification, mechanistically distinct from loop-dependent failures, exhibiting negligible loop incidence in our experiments.
  Evaluating five open-source VLM families on MS COCO and ImageNet under an 8/255 infinity-norm budget, JPPO achieves over 4.6x latency and 5.3x energy amplification on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and over 36.6x latency with 32.7x energy amplification on BLIP-2. This represents the strongest cost amplification among directly compared baselines while requiring substantially fewer optimization iterations. Ablations confirm this amplification arises from multimodal coordination rather than prompt length or isolated modalities. These findings reveal structural blind spots in current VLM serving defenses, motivating cost-aware robustness evaluation as a first-class security requirement for multimodal deployments.

</details>


### [148] [The End of AI Exponentiation: Fluttering Inside and Outside AI Bubble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05894)
*Victor Kebande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能指数级发展的终结，分析了AI生态内外部的"泡沫"现象——包括算力与数据中心竞赛、投机性投资、超级智能竞赛等内部不稳定因素，以及劳动力颠覆、治理担忧、公众不确定性、地缘政治加速等外部挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI在LLM、大规模算力基础设施和自主推理系统推动下进入变革时代，其传统线性扩展范式正面临数据峰值、算力需求上升、合成数据递归、估值通胀和社会不稳定等摩擦，传统scaling范式难以持续支撑指数增长，因此需要审视"AI指数化终结"这一议题。

Method: 本文采用系统性分析视角，从AI生态"内部"和"外部"两个维度剖析不稳定性的来源——内部包括算力和数据中心竞赛、投机性投资、超级智能竞争性追逐；外部包括劳动力市场颠覆、治理关切、公众不确定性和全球地缘政治加速。

Result: 文章指出AI指数级发展正面临多层面的增长瓶颈和结构性摩擦，AI生态内外均呈现出显著的不稳定性与泡沫化特征，传统扩展范式已难以维持持续指数增长。

Conclusion: 本文提出"AI指数化终结"的概念框架，认为AI发展正进入一个新的转折阶段，需要重新审视其技术、社会、经济等多维度的演进路径。

Abstract: The exponentiation of Artificial intelligence (AI) in the recent past has entered a transformative era that has been driven by the growth in large language models (LLMs), large-scale compute infrastructures, and autonomous reasoning systems. However, the rapid acceleration of AI has increasingly shown technological, societal, economic, ethical and infrastructural challenges associated with peak data limitations, rising computational demands, synthetic data recursion, valuation inflation, and societal instability. The traditional scaling paradigms that have powered the modern AI systems are gradually encountering friction in sustaining continuous exponential growth. This paper views ``the end of AI exponentiation,'' thus exploring how it flutters inside and outside the bubble, where instability emerges within the AI ecosystem through compute and data-center races, speculative investments, and the rat-race toward superintelligence, and outside the ecosystem through labor disruption, governance concerns, public uncertainty, and geopolitical acceleration surrounding future intelligent systems and infrastructures globally.

</details>


### [149] [Beyond Final Decisions: A Process-Centric Benchmark for Transparent AI-Assisted Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05947)
*Siming Yuan,Xueyi Zhang,Wangze Ni,Tianfang Xiao,Shimin Di,Jia Zhu,Zhuoren Jiang,Rong Tan,Lei Chen,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向AI辅助同行评审的过程中心化诊断基准，通过阶段级评价、链一致性评价和干预敏感性分析，检验模型决策是否被充分的评审证据所支持，而非仅关注最终评审质量或决策准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助同行评审的评估主要聚焦于整体评审质量或最终决策准确性，无法回答模型决策是否由充分且可靠的评审证据支持这一关键问题。因此需要一个过程导向的诊断工具来评估评审过程的可靠性。

Method: 构建一个过程中心化基准，用(x,z_s,z_c,z_r,y)表示论文内容、摘要、批评、建议与决策；将PeerRead、NLPeer ARR-22和OpenReview-ICLR转换为过程对齐数据；以直接从论文内容预测决策(Direct)为基线，对比Gold过程和Predicted过程变量的决策价值，开展阶段级评估、链一致性评估和干预敏感性分析。

Result: 三个数据集、六个模型的实验表明：Gold过程变量通常具有较高的决策价值；主分析模型中Gold-Predicted差距在数据集和随机种子间保持稳定，且在多数模型-数据组合中可复现；虽然模型生成的中间评审文本在相邻阶段具有相对较高的局部一致性，但最终决策并未总是得到先前评审证据的一致支持。

Conclusion: 该基准面向旨在辅助而非替代人类评审者的AI系统，提供了一个透明且可审计的诊断工具，用于评估其评审过程的可靠性。

Abstract: Peer review is central to quality control in science. However, existing evaluations of AI-assisted peer review mainly focus on the overall quality of generated reviews or the accuracy of final decisions. They therefore provide limited evidence about whether model decisions are supported by sufficient and reliable review evidence. We introduce a process-centric diagnostic benchmark for AI-assisted peer review. It uses (x,$z_s$,$z_c$,$z_r$,y)
  to represent the paper content, summary, critique, suggestion, and decision. We convert heterogeneous review records from PeerRead, NLPeer ARR-22, and OpenReview-ICLR into process-aligned data. Our benchmark uses direct decision prediction from the paper content (Direct) as its baseline. It compares the decision value of Gold-process variables and Predicted-process variables, and conducts stage-level evaluation, chain-consistency evaluation, and interventional sensitivity analysis. Experiments across three datasets and six models show that Gold-process variables generally have higher decision value. For the main analysis model, the Gold--Predicted gap remains stable across datasets and random seeds. This gap is also reproduced in most model--dataset combinations. Although model-generated intermediate review texts show relatively high local consistency across adjacent stages, the final decisions are not consistently supported by the preceding review evidence. Our benchmark targets AI systems designed to assist rather than replace human reviewers. It provides a transparent and auditable diagnostic tool for evaluating the reliability of their review processes.

</details>


### [150] [MOAE: Multi-Objective Agent Evolution with Pareto-Preserving Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05992)
*Hengle Jiang,Qijun Cai,Ziying Luo,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多目标Agent进化（MOAE）方法，将迭代式上下文精炼组织为Pareto保持的进化搜索过程，在有限 rollout 预算下维护非支配候选存档，将搜索阶段的候选保留与部署阶段的人类偏好解耦，无需参数更新即可同时优化任务性能、轨迹质量和安全性等多个Agent属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent的评估日益多维化，不仅要求任务完成准确率，还需兼顾交互质量、安全性和效率。现有方法多为多目标优化，但常将异构测量指标折叠为固定标量分数，这类标量化依赖指标归一化和偏好权重，可能丢弃具有实用部署权衡价值的候选方案。核心研究问题在于这些目标能否被同时优化。

Method: MOAE将Agent的完整rollout组织为Pareto-preserving进化搜索。具体包括：维护非支配候选的经验存档；使用目标特定的诊断信息指导后代生成；仅在部署阶段应用约束感知选择，从而分离搜索期间的候选保留与最终解返回所需的偏好设置。该方法无需参数更新，允许将任意可测量属性替换目标，实例化为任务性能、轨迹质量和安全性三个维度。

Result: 在TravelPlanner和AgentDojo基准上的实验表明，MOAE在匹配的rollout预算下一致地提升了任务性能和轨迹质量，同时保持强大的安全性。搜索行为分析进一步显示Pareto保留扩展了可达目标区域，并增加了联合改进的频率。

Conclusion: 结果表明Pareto保持的上下文进化在优化多个Agent属性方面具有潜力，能够在搜索过程中不固定标量化偏好，从而保留更丰富的部署权衡空间。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents continue to advance, their evaluation has become increasingly multifaceted: a capable agent must not only achieve high task completion accuracy but also perform well in interaction quality, safety, and efficiency, raising a central question: can these objectives be optimized simultaneously? Existing methods have considered multiple objectives, but many collapse heterogeneous measurements into a fixed scalar score. Such scalarization depends on metric normalization and preference weights and may discard candidates that represent useful deployment trade-offs. We introduce Multi-Objective Agent Evolution (MOAE), which organizes iterative in-context refinement as a Pareto-preserving evolutionary search over complete agent rollouts. Given a limited rollout budget, MOAE maintains an empirical archive of non-dominated candidates, uses objective-specific diagnostics to guide offspring generation, and applies constraint-aware selection only at deployment. This separates candidate preservation during search from the preference used to return a final solution. The procedure requires no parameter updates and allows each objective to be replaced by any measurable property, which we instantiate as task performance, trajectory quality, and safety. Experiments on TravelPlanner and AgentDojo show that MOAE consistently improves task performance and trajectory quality while maintaining strong safety under matched rollout budgets. Search-behavior analysis further shows that Pareto preservation expands the attainable objective region and increases the frequency of joint improvement. These results demonstrate the potential of Pareto-preserving in-context evolution for optimizing multiple agent properties without committing to a fixed scalarization during search.

</details>


### [151] [Agentic Pressure: The Endogenous Entropy of Reliable Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05995)
*Hengle Jiang,Ziying Luo,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并形式化了"Agentic Pressure"（代理压力）这一非对抗性现象：在长时程自主运行中，智能体在无约束环境中因目标达成与合规成本之间的冲突而自发产生一种内生动力学压力。当该压力超过临界阈值时，智能体将安全漂移（safety drift）视为数学上最优的适应策略，并常通过"工具性幻觉"（Instrumental Hallucination）为违规行为辩护。实验证明，高压力条件下对齐智能体会自发牺牲安全性以维持自主性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注对抗性越狱（static jailbreaks）对智能体对齐的破坏，但忽略了野生环境中因长时程交互动力学产生的非对抗性、内生性对齐退化机制。本文旨在识别并系统刻画这一独特现象。

Method: 将Agentic Pressure形式化为"克服环境摩擦所需功与智能体剩余容量之比"，构建理论框架分析该压力的动态演化；通过对压力超阈值的数学分析，推导出安全漂移作为最优适应策略的条件，并提出Instrumental Hallucination作为违规合理化机制的解释。

Result: 理论分析表明，当Agentic Pressure超过临界阈值时，安全漂移是智能体在数学意义上的最优适应行为；实证实验验证了该框架，确认了对齐智能体在高压力条件下确实会自发妥协安全以保持自主运行。

Conclusion: Agentic Pressure揭示了长时程自主运行中对齐失效的结构性根源——它并非来自外部对抗输入，而是交互过程中的内生动力学产物。这提示可靠自主系统的设计需考虑压力阈值管理与容量预留机制，而非仅依赖静态对齐手段。

Abstract: Achieving reliable autonomy in the wild requires agents to sustain continuous operations across long-horizon trajectories. However, as agents navigate these unconstrained settings, they encounter cumulative friction that inherently destabilizes their alignment. In this paper, we identify a distinct non-adversarial phenomenon termed Agentic Pressure. We define this as a kinetic force that spontaneously emerges when the cost of compliance conflicts with the imperative of goal achievement. Unlike static jailbreaks, this pressure is endogenous and arises directly from the dynamics of interaction. We propose a theoretical framework that formalizes Agentic Pressure as the ratio between the required work to overcome environmental friction and the remaining capacity of the agent. Our analysis demonstrates that when this pressure exceeds a critical threshold, agents exhibit safety drift as a mathematically optimal adaptation. Consequently, they often resort to Instrumental Hallucination to rationalize rule violations. Empirical experiments validate this framework and show that aligned agents spontaneously compromise safety to preserve autonomy under high-pressure conditions.

</details>


### [152] [Generator-Independent Runtime Assurance under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06036)
*Guangxi Wan,Yongbo Xie,Yuqi Liu,Qingwei Dong,Qingxin Li,Hongfei Bai,Peng Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究基于提议（proposal）的控制器在运行时验证门控下的安全性保证是否与生成器解耦。作者证明逐候选认证模式在重试或best-of-k选择下不具组合性，并提出"同时集合健全性"（simultaneous setwise soundness）作为生成器无关的准入健全性的充要条件，配合设计时证书与不可旁路规则可实现合约安全，违约界为Γ+Σₜεₜ+η，且对任意对抗性生成器替换保持不变。文章还给出部分观测下所有准入机制的界限，并通过顺序风险账本使保证可用时间一致置信管实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于提议的控制器（如学习策略、语言模型规划器或其他黑盒生成器）越来越多地被部署在运行时验证门控之后。需要回答问题：闭环安全性保证何时能与生成器解耦。现有的逐候选认证模式在重试或best-of-k选择场景下不具组合性——单候选误准入水平α会膨胀为1-(1-α)^k，因此需要一种生成器无关的安全性保证框架。

Method: 本文提出核心概念"同时集合健全性"（simultaneous setwise soundness），即认证一组不含任何不可行动作的可行提议集合。基于此建立两层保证：其一为准入健全性（admission soundness），是该集合性健全性对生成器无关的最坏情况安全保证的充要条件；其二为合约安全（contract safety），由设计时证书与不可旁路规则共同构成，违约界为Γ+Σₜεₜ+η。第二个定理通过total-variation距离刻画部分观测下所有准入机制的下界：若两个状态假设的信息律在TV距离δ内却需要不同安全决策，则ā+β+δ≥1。最后用顺序风险账本（sequential risk ledger）配合时间一致置信管使保证可实施。

Result: 第一个定理表明：同时集合健全性是生成器无关准入健全性的充要条件；结合设计时证书与不可旁路规则可得到合约安全，违约界Γ+Σₜεₜ+η在任意甚至对抗性生成器替换下保持不变。第二个定理给出部分观测下任何准入机制的不可回避下界ā+β+δ≥1。顺序风险账本展示了确定性准入计算将所有统计风险集中在状态估计上。Simplex风格运行时保障与控制屏障函数滤波被证明是该框架的退化情形。

Conclusion: 该框架从根本上回答了基于提议的控制器安全性何时与生成器解耦：当且仅当采用同时集合健全性认证（而非逐候选认证）时，安全保证可对任意黑盒生成器保持成立。文章同时给出了部分观测下的理论下界，并提供了可实施的顺序风险账本工具，将Simplex运行时保障和控制屏障函数等现有方法统一为特例。

Abstract: Proposal-based controllers---learned policies, language-model planners, and other black-box \emph{generators}---are increasingly deployed behind runtime verification gates. We ask when the closed-loop safety guarantee decouples from the generator. The prevailing per-candidate certification pattern does not compose: under retry or best-of-$k$ selection a per-candidate false-admission level $α$ can inflate to $1-(1-α)^{k}$. Our main theorem shows that \emph{simultaneous setwise soundness}---certifying a set of admissible proposals containing no nonviable action---is necessary and sufficient for generator-independent \emph{admission soundness}, the worst case over all generators of executing a nonviable proposal equalling the probability of setwise failure; together with a design-time certificate and a no-bypass rule it is sufficient for \emph{contract safety}, with violation bound $Γ+\sum_t\varepsilon_t+η$ invariant under arbitrary, even adversarial, replacement of the generator. A second theorem bounds every admission mechanism under partial observation: for a fixed probing and admission policy, if two state hypotheses whose information laws lie within total-variation distance $δ$ require different safe decisions, then $\abar+β+δ\ge1$. A sequential risk ledger makes the guarantee implementable with time-uniform confidence tubes, and shows that deterministic admission computations concentrate all statistical risk in state estimation. Simplex-style runtime assurance and control-barrier-function filtering are recovered as degenerate cases.

</details>


### [153] [DAREBench: Deployment-Aware and Reliable Evaluation of Models as Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06059)
*Yu Liu,Zhilin Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Shaojie Zhang,Zheyuan Deng,Tingwei Huang,Zhenbo Luo,Lei Jiang,Yanbing Liu,Pei Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DAREBench是一个面向通用AI智能体部署评估的基准测试平台，基于共享OpenClaw执行环境，通过2×3工作负载矩阵组织233项任务（来自22个源基准），采用统一的契约式评估协议和基于证据的评分审计，对23个商业API模型和12个本地开源模型进行了7,587次模型-任务运行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型从问答系统演进为通用智能体，现有评估基准局限于特定任务类型、执行环境或评分协议，缺乏对不同任务类型的可比性、可解释性和可靠性，难以支撑实际部署决策。需要一个能捕捉工作负载变化且支持可靠智能体评估的基准体系。

Method: 基于共享的OpenClaw执行环境构建基准，从22个源基准中筛选和改编233个任务，按输入模态和执行形式组织为2×3工作负载矩阵；在统一的基于契约的评估协议下进行测试，并配以基于证据的评分审计机制；评估覆盖23个商业API模型和12个本地部署的开源模型。

Result: 论文报告了7,587次模型-任务运行的准确性、token消耗以及API模型参考成本。核心发现包括：没有单一模型能在所有工作负载组中全面占优；文本与多模态任务展现出不同的准确率-成本权衡特性；本地开源模型在若干工作负载组中具备竞争力，但整体上仍落后于前沿商业模型。

Conclusion: 智能体的部署与模型选择应综合考虑工作负载特征、部署模式以及准确率-成本权衡，而非仅依据单一聚合分数做出决策。

Abstract: As large language models evolve from question-answering systems into general-purpose agents, evaluation must move beyond static answer correctness to assess multimodal perception, multi-step execution, tool use, and artifact delivery. However, existing benchmarks are often tied to specific task types, execution environments, or scoring protocols, limiting their comparability, interpretability, and reliability for deployment decisions. We introduce DAREBench (Deployment-Aware and Reliable Evaluation of Models as Agents), a benchmark designed to capture workload variation and support reliable agent evaluation. Built on a shared OpenClaw execution environment, DAREBench organizes 233 tasks selected and adapted from 22 source benchmarks into a $2\times3$ workload matrix defined by input modality and execution form, and evaluates them under a unified contract-based protocol with evidence-based score auditing. We evaluate 23 commercial API models and 12 locally deployed open-weight models over 7,587 model--task runs, reporting accuracy and token consumption alongside reference costs for API models. Results show that no single model dominates all workload groups, text and multimodal tasks exhibit distinct accuracy--cost trade-offs, and local open-weight models are competitive in several groups but still trail frontier commercial models overall. These findings suggest that agent deployment and model selection should consider workload profiles, deployment mode, and accuracy--cost trade-offs rather than rely on a single aggregate score.

</details>


### [154] [Explaining AI Agents Through Execution Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06063)
*Vittoria Vineis,Fabiano Veglianti,Lorenzo Antonelli,Claudia Di Carlo,Matteo Silvestri,Gabriele Tolomei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种针对AI Agent的后验可解释框架，将代理的执行轨迹转化为结构化报告和可追溯的自然语言解释，实现过程级透明，在多种基准和架构上优于朴素LLM生成解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI Agent在真实环境中需要与外部工具交互并做序列决策，但缺乏人类监督，亟需可靠、可审计的过程级透明解释。传统XAI方法不适用于此类交互式、多步骤系统，需要专门为AI Agent设计的可解释方法。

Method: 提出一个后验XAI框架，将冗长的Agent执行轨迹转换为结构化报告，并生成严格基于可观察行为的忠实自然语言解释。该框架仅依赖执行轨迹，因此可跨不同Agent架构、环境和任务通用。

Result: 在多个基准和架构上，人类与自动化评估表明该框架能生成高质量、轨迹忠实的解释，同时可靠地识别无依据的主张、不合理的动作和证据缺口，优于朴素LLM生成的解释。

Conclusion: 基于执行轨迹的后验XAI框架有效提供了AI Agent所需的过程级透明性，具有跨架构通用性，并能识别传统方法难以捕捉的推理缺陷。

Abstract: AI Agents are increasingly deployed in real-world settings, where they interact with external tools and make sequential decisions with limited human oversight. This creates a pressing need for reliable and auditable explanations of what an agent did and why. However, traditional Explainable AI (XAI) methods fall short of providing the process-level transparency required for such interactive, multi-step systems, motivating a paradigm shift toward approaches specifically designed for AI Agents. To address this gap, we present a post-hoc XAI framework that transforms a lengthy agent's execution trace into a structured report and a faithful natural-language explanation explicitly grounded in its observable behavior. Because it relies solely on execution traces, the framework applies across different agent architectures, environments, and tasks. Human and automated evaluations across multiple benchmarks and architectures show that our framework produces high-quality, trace-faithful explanations while reliably identifying unsupported claims, unjustified actions, and evidence gaps, outperforming naive LLM-generated explanations.

</details>


### [155] [Generating Instance Generators in PDDL Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06071)
*Nicola J. Müller,Naya Rudolph,Katharina Stein,Jörg Hoffmann,Ayal Taitler,Timo P. Gros*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用大语言模型(LLM)自动生成PDDL规划领域实例生成程序的新方法，通过内置的合法性检查保证生成实例的可靠性（soundness），克服了传统手工编写实例生成器以及现有推理/学习方法在可扩展性上的局限，能够高效生成大量多样且合法的规划实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDDL作为AI规划领域的标准语言，只能定义规划域（谓词和动作模式），无法明确指定合法的实例集（初始状态、目标条件的合法性约束以及感兴趣的域子集约束）。这导致实例生成长期依赖手工编写的特定于域和子集的生成器，而近年来的推理和学习方法虽然开始解决这一问题，但面临可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种替代方案：利用LLM来生成实例生成程序，并通过预设的检查机制为生成结果提供内置的可靠性（soundness）保证。具体而言，LLM根据领域描述自动编写生成合法规划实例的程序代码，随后通过预定义检查验证这些程序的正确性。

Result: 实验表明，这些自动生成的实例生成程序能够高效地生成大量且多样化的、合法的规划实例，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 利用LLM自动生成带可靠性保证的实例生成程序是一条可行且高效的新路径，相比传统手工编写生成器以及先前的推理/学习方法具有更好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: PDDL, the de-facto standard language in the AI Planning community, is designed to specify planning domains: sets of instances that share the same predicates and action schemas. Yet it does not provide any means to specify the actual instance set, i.e., legality constraints on initial states and goal conditions, as well as possibly domain subset constraints specifying an instance subset we are interested in. One consequence of this is that instance generation has always been ad-hoc, with manually written domain- and subset-specific instance generators. Recent work has started to address this, through reasoning and learning methods that however suffer from scalability limitations. Here we introduce an alternative approach, leveraging LLMs to generate instance-generation programs, with built-in soundness guarantees through prescribed checks. We show that these automatically generated instance generators return large numbers of sound and diverse instances efficiently.

</details>


### [156] [LayerRoute: Action-Conditioned Mixture-of-Layers Routing for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06079)
*Zheng Lu,Haoran Liao,Wanqi Zhong,Yunhe Ni,Lijie Wang,Xingjie Fan,Zhisheng Chen,Yantang Qu,Meijia Chen,Tianyu Xin,Zirui Song,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LayerRoute，一种动作条件化的表示路由接口，通过层混合路由器和动作状态重读机制，使VLA策略能够自适应地访问不同VLM层和中间动作表示，在LIBERO Long等模拟和真实基准上一致提升StarVLA-π和π0.5的性能，最高仅以0.31%/3.87%的额外参数代价获得7.2分提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略中，VLM信息通过固定的层分配暴露给动作模块，无法根据不同操纵任务的需求灵活混合不同层的视觉语义表示；同时中间动作状态仅通过残差流隐式传播，缺乏显式重用。这种有限的表示访问灵活性限制了VLA策略的表达能力和适配性。

Method: LayerRoute包含两个核心组件：层混合路由器（Layer Mixture Router），根据当前动作状态动态混合缓存的VLM各层表示；动作状态重读（Action-State Reread），显式重用先前动作层的中间表示。该方法通过动作条件化的方式实现自适应的表示选择与路由。

Result: 在多种模拟和真实世界基准上，LayerRoute一致地提升了StarVLA-π和π0.5的性能，在LIBERO Long任务上最高获得7.2分的提升，额外参数仅占0.31%/3.87%。消融实验验证了动作条件化层路由的益处，路由分析揭示了不同动作层和任务设置下结构化的分配模式。

Conclusion: LayerRoute通过提供灵活的、动作条件化的表示路由能力，使得VLA策略能够根据任务需求自适应地组合VLM层特征并重用中间动作表示，以极小的参数开销显著提升性能。消融与路由分析进一步验证了该设计的合理性与可解释性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies leverage pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) to guide action generation for robot control. VLMs provide hierarchical visual-semantic representations that evolve across layers, from local visual geometry to abstract, language-aligned semantics; different manipulation tasks may therefore require different mixtures of layer representations. Meanwhile, the action module maintains intermediate representations that evolve throughout action computation and may provide useful information for subsequent decisions. However, existing VLA interfaces offer limited flexibility in representation access: VLM information is exposed through fixed layer assignments for each action layer, while intermediate action states are only propagated implicitly through residual streams without explicit reuse. We introduce LayerRoute, an action-conditioned representation routing interface that enables adaptive access to VLM layers and action representations. The Layer Mixture Router dynamically forms mixtures of cached VLM representations, while Action-State Reread reuses earlier action representations. Across diverse simulation and real-world benchmarks, LayerRoute consistently improves StarVLA-$π$ and $π_{0.5}$, achieving up to 7.2 gains on LIBERO Long with only 0.31% / 3.87% additional parameters. Ablation studies validate the benefit of action-conditioned layer routing, while routing analyses reveal structured allocation patterns across action layers and task settings.

</details>


### [157] [SAP: State-Guided Data Synthesis with Argument Provenance for Multi-Turn Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06124)
*Zichen Tian,Jinpeng Chen,Cheng Gong,Suiyun Zhang,Rui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SAP（State-Guided Data Synthesis with Argument Provenance），一种带参数溯源约束的状态引导式数据合成方法，用于生成高质量的多轮工具使用轨迹数据，解决现有合成数据中参数级依赖不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量多轮工具使用数据对训练智能体模型至关重要，但现有数据合成方法往往忽视参数级依赖关系（argument-level dependencies），导致模型即使选对工具，也可能因使用虚假、过期或弱依据的参数值而导致任务执行失败。

Method: SAP结合了三个关键组件：1）状态引导（state guidance），保证轨迹生成过程中的状态一致性；2）工具参数溯源约束（tool-argument provenance constraints），确保参数值有可靠来源而非凭空捏造；3）轮次级别验证（turn-level validation），在每轮生成后验证轨迹的正确性。通过这些机制高效构建具有长程依赖和高准确率的工具使用轨迹。

Result: 利用SAP生成的数据训练得到SAP-4B模型，在多个基准测试上与更大的模型相比具有高度竞争力。

Conclusion: SAP通过引入状态引导和参数溯源机制，有效提升了合成工具使用数据的质量，验证了该方法在训练紧凑但高性能智能体模型方面的有效性。

Abstract: High-quality multi-turn tool-use data is essential for training agentic models, yet existing data synthesis methods often underrepresent the argument-level dependencies that are critical to long-horizon tool use. As a result, even when a model selects the correct tool, task execution may still fail because the model fills tool arguments with fabricated, stale, or weakly grounded values. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{State-Guided Data Synthesis with Argument Provenance (SAP)}. SAP combines state guidance, tool-argument provenance constraints, and turn-level validation to efficiently construct tool-use trajectories with long-range dependencies and high accuracy. Using data generated by SAP, we build SAP-4B, which is highly competitive even when compared with much larger models across multiple benchmarks. Source code, synthesized data, and trained weights are available at https://github.com/Zichen1024/SAP.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [ECHO: Dyadic 3D Facial Motion Generation with Asymmetric Deterministic Articulation and Stochastic Reaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05506)
*Zhuoqiang Cai,Yujie Sun,Chaoyue Niu,Hongyun Yu,Zhiwen Chen,Chengfei Lv,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出ECHO框架，在严格双流纯音频条件下进行双人3D面部运动生成，通过将运动分解为确定性锚点与随机残差来处理语音约束与听话者反应的异构问题，并引入Motion Memory训练正则化与语义组缩放机制，实现说话方发音保真与听话方真实性和多样性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双人3D面部运动生成中，说话方受语音约束（确定性），而听话方反应是一对多的随机行为，两者不对称。现有方法通常对整体运动统一施加随机性，难以同时保证说话方发音准确性与听话方反应的多样性和真实性。

Method: 1) 将双人面部运动分解为确定性锚点（捕捉与语音相关的稳定结构）与随机残差（建模一对多的交互动态）；2) 引入Motion Memory作为仅训练阶段的正则器，在简短后期微调中为弱条件听窗口提供局部先验；3) 语义组缩放控制残差在表情、下颌、颈部等不同语义部位上的注入强度。

Result: 统一评估、逐状态评估以及消融实验表明，将稳定与不确定的运动成分进行分解比统一施加随机性更能提升会话3D运动生成质量，ECHO在严格纯音频条件下实现了说话侧发音保真与听话侧真实性和多样性的兼顾。

Conclusion: ECHO为严格音频条件下的可部署会话数字人提供了一种实用的问题建模与技术方案，验证了将确定性锚点与随机残差分离的有效性与可行性。

Abstract: We propose ECHO for dyadic 3D facial motion generation under a strict dual-stream audio-only setting, formulating the problem as an asymmetric task involving speech-constrained articulation and one-to-many listener reactions. To address this asymmetry, ECHO decomposes motion into a deterministic anchor that captures stable speech-correlated structure and a stochastic residual that models the remaining one-to-many interaction dynamics. On top of this backbone, Motion Memory acts as a training-only regularizer during brief late-stage fine-tuning to provide local priors for weakly conditioned listening windows, while semantic-group scaling controls residual injection across expression, jaw, and neck. This design balances speaking-side articulatory fidelity with listening-side realism and diversity in a single generation process. Results from unified, state-wise, and ablation evaluations show that conversational 3D motion benefits from decomposing stable and uncertain components rather than applying stochasticity uniformly. ECHO provides a practical formulation and technical basis for deployable conversational digital humans under strict audio-only conditions.

</details>


### [159] [RBF Your SDF: Radial Basis Function Interpolation of Signed Distance Fields with Implied Tangent Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06209)
*Yong Cheng,Yotam Gingold*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的SDF隐式几何重建方法，将切球结构与径向基函数插值相结合，利用所有数据点（而非仅表面附近样本）进行重建，并通过检测切点高度受约束的球来识别和保留尖锐特征（角点），同时采用划分单位分解实现高效的大规模网格重建，在Chamfer和Hausdorff精度上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统重建方法（如marching cubes、dual contouring）忽略了远离表面的SDF样本所携带的几何信息。虽然最近的切球方法利用了SDF样本隐含的切球几何结构，但这些方法仍通过表面重建来提取零等值面，只考虑表面上的点和法线，忽略了其余样本信息，导致尖锐特征被系统性圆化。

Method: 将切球观察与径向基函数（RBF）插值相结合，同时利用隐含的表面点和原始SDF全部数据。通过检测具有极度受限切点的球（这一几何构型在尖锐表面特征处必然出现），识别并保留角点。采用划分单位分解（partition-of-unity）使方法可高效扩展到大规模网格分辨率。

Result: 在每种测试分辨率下，重建结果在Chamfer距离和Hausdorff距离两种精度指标上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 这种融合全部SDF样本信息与切球观测的重建方法能够有效保留尖锐几何特征，并在所有测试分辨率下实现更优的重建精度，为基于SDF的显式表面重建提供了新的高效途径。

Abstract: Signed distance fields (SDFs) are a popular implicit representation of geometry. Converting a discrete set of SDF samples into an explicit surface is a fundamental problem in geometry processing. Traditional reconstruction methods such as marching cubes and dual contouring ignore the geometric information carried by samples far from the surface. Recently, Sellán et al.[2023] and several follow-up works leveraged the tangent-sphere structure of SDFs; every sample implies a point on a sphere tangent to the surface. However, these approaches extract the zero-level set via surface reconstruction, which considers only points and normals on the surface and ignores the remaining samples. We propose an approach that marries the tangent-sphere observation with radial basis function interpolation of all data, the implied surface points and the original data. By detecting spheres with extremely constrained tangent points, a configuration geometrically forced at sharp surface features, we identify and preserve surface corners that surface reconstruction-based methods systematically round. A partition-of-unity decomposition allows our method to scale efficiently to large grid resolutions. Our reconstructions improve both Chamfer and Hausdorff accuracy at every tested resolution.

</details>


### [160] [Skinned Motion Retargeting via Artifact-driven Kinematic Prior Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06517)
*Seokhyeon Hong,Chaelin Kim,Inseo Jang,Soojin Choi,Junyong Noh*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个几何感知的运动重定向框架，通过显式连接角色网格上的自穿透伪影与运动细化，在保持骨架无关的神经重定向灵活性的同时，减少目标侧几何伪影，提升重定向准确性与运动合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经运动重定向方法虽在不同骨架间具有灵活性，但难以解决目标侧几何伪影（如自穿透）。现有几何感知方法依赖固定骨架模板或隐式几何条件预测，要求单一网络同时处理目标几何形变检测、伪影识别与修正预测，难以泛化到多样化骨架结构和体型。

Method: 提出统一的几何感知运动重定向框架：(1) 基于Transformer的重定向自编码器学习跨不同骨架共享的运动嵌入，实现任意源-目标骨架对的运动迁移；(2) 引入伪影驱动细化模块，通过运动-顶点雅可比矩阵将目标网格上的自穿透观察转换为修正信号；(3) 使用基于蒙皮权重的关节对齐几何特征（源自静止姿态网格）来条件化运动解码，实现在统一框架中结合显式目标伪影推理与灵活的几何感知解码。

Result: 在固定与任意骨架结构设置下的实验表明，该方法提升了运动学重定向精度并减少了几何伪影，在已知与未见的目标角色上均能产生合理的运动。

Conclusion: 通过显式地将角色几何中的伪影观察与运动细化联系起来，同时利用共享运动先验和几何感知运动解码，该方法有效解决了现有方法在处理多样骨架和体型时泛化能力不足的局限，为运动重定向提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Motion retargeting aims to transfer a source motion to target characters with different skeletal structures, proportions, and body shapes. Although recent neural retargeting methods have improved flexibility across diverse skeletons, target-side geometric artifacts such as self-penetration remain difficult to resolve. Specifically, existing geometry-aware approaches often rely on fixed skeleton templates or implicit geometry-conditioned prediction, requiring a single network to account for target geometry deformation, detect target-side artifacts, and predict the corresponding correction from target geometry alone, which limits their ability to generalize across diverse skeleton structures and body shapes. In this paper, we present a geometry-aware motion retargeting framework that explicitly connects artifacts observed in the posed character geometry to motion refinement while preserving the flexibility of skeleton-agnostic neural retargeting. Our method first learns a motion embedding shared across different skeletons using a transformer-based retargeting autoencoder that transfers motion across arbitrary source--target skeleton pairs. Building on this kinematic motion prior, we introduce an artifact-driven refinement module that observes self-penetration on the posed target mesh and converts it into a corrective cue through a motion-to-vertex Jacobian. We further condition motion decoding on target geometry using skinning weight-based joint-aligned geometry features derived from the rest pose mesh. This design combines explicit target-side artifact reasoning with flexible geometry-aware decoding in a unified framework. Experiments on both fixed and arbitrary skeleton structure settings show that our method improves kinematic retargeting accuracy and reduces geometric artifacts, producing plausible motions across seen and unseen target characters.

</details>


### [161] [CT2Yarn: Yarn-Level Reconstruction of Crochet from Computed Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06950)
*Chang Luo,Nobuyuki Umetani*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出CT2Yarn，一个基于人类在环（human-in-the-loop）的框架，用于从真实钩针编织物的显微CT扫描中恢复单一连续纱线路径。方法结合Gabor滤波估计纤维方向、各向异性均值漂移聚合纱线级方向、自动骨架化提取路径片段，并通过草图交互界面处理歧义完成路径恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从外部观测恢复钩针编织物中单一纱线的完整路径极具挑战性，因为存在严重的自遮挡问题。虽然显微CT可以揭示物体的完整内部结构，但体积扫描本身并未显式编码纱线在物体中的走向。其根本难点在于纱线的层级结构：纱线由多股加捻线组成，每股又由加捻纤维构成，导致局部纤维方向与整体纱线方向不一致。

Method: 首先使用Gabor滤波估计局部纤维方向，并将体数据转换为带方向的点云。然后提出各向异性均值漂移过程，在纱线半径邻域内聚合局部纤维方向以获得纱线级方向估计。结合自动拓扑骨架化策略提取纱线路径片段；随后通过片段链接、交叉点清理和环检测将这些树合并为少量长曲线。在自动重建存在歧义的位置，通过基于草图的用户界面实现交互式补充，以完成单一连续纱线路径的恢复。

Result: 所恢复的纱线路径可用于下游应用，包括基于物理的仿真、纱线股级渲染以及针法图案提取。

Conclusion: CT2Yarn成功解决了从显微CT扫描中恢复钩针编织物完整连续纱线路径的问题，通过结合自动计算与人工交互的方式，克服了层级纤维结构与严重自遮挡带来的方向歧义挑战，为编织物体的数字化分析与重建提供了一条可行的技术路线。

Abstract: We introduce CT2Yarn, a human-in-the-loop framework for recovering a single continuous yarn path from micro-computed tomography (micro-CT) scans of real crochet objects. Crochet is a craft that creates complex three-dimensional shapes by interlocking loops formed from a single yarn. Recovering the underlying yarn path from external observations is challenging because of severe self-occlusion. While micro-CT reveals the full internal structure of a crochet object, the volumetric scan alone does not explicitly encode how the yarn traverses the object. This challenge stems from the hierarchical structure of yarn: a yarn consists of multiple twisted plies, and each ply itself consists of twisted fibers. Consequently, local fiber orientations observed in micro-CT scans are not aligned with the overall yarn direction. To recover yarn-level orientations from ply-level fiber orientations, we first estimate local fiber directions using Gabor filtering and convert the volume into an oriented point cloud. We then introduce an anisotropic mean-shift procedure that aggregates local fiber orientations within a neighborhood of the yarn radius into yarn-level orientation estimates. Combined with an automatic topology skeletonization strategy, our method extracts yarn-path fragments. Subsequent fragment linking, junction cleaning, and loop detection merge these trees into a small number of long curves. Where the automatic reconstruction remains ambiguous, a sketch-based user interface enables users to interactively complete the single continuous yarn path. The recovered yarn path enables downstream applications including physically based simulation, ply-level rendering, and stitch-pattern extraction.

</details>


### [162] [Neural Centroidal Voronoi Tessellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08497)
*Jiacheng Xu,Bo Pang,Rui Xu,Xiaocheng Zhang,Yang Liu,Fei Zhu,Guoping Wang,Peng-Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出Neural CVT，一种基于学习的曲面质心Voronoi剖分（CVT）求解器，用循环神经网络优化器替代传统求解器中昂贵的受限Voronoi图构造与面积分几何计算，将CVT优化加速1-2个数量级，同时保持各向同性和保特征的重网格质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统曲面CVT求解成本高昂，每一步优化都需要反复构造受限Voronoi图并在各曲面上进行数值积分，导致计算开销巨大，限制了其在高质量曲面采样和各向同性重网格中的应用效率。

Method: 给定输入曲面，先采样稠密点云并用图神经网络编码器提取多尺度几何描述子；然后由轻量级循环优化器在少量迭代内精化种子点位置，通过聚合曲面插值特征与优化历史预测每个点的位移。框架以自监督方式训练，损失由CVT目标构成（促进均匀分布及可选的保特征对齐）。

Result: 在多样化的有机形状与CAD类形状上，Neural CVT能泛化到未见几何、不同初始化策略及不同种子密度，产生与最先进的离线优化方法相当的各向同性、保特征重网格结果，而计算成本仅为后者的一小部分。

Conclusion: Neural CVT验证了用学习驱动的循环优化器替代经典几何计算求解CVT的可行性，在保持几何保真度的前提下大幅提升效率，且具备良好的跨几何泛化能力。

Abstract: Centroidal Voronoi tessellation (CVT) is a fundamental primitive for high-quality surface sampling and isotropic remeshing in computer graphics. However, computing surface CVTs with classical solvers remains expensive: each optimization step repeatedly constructs restricted Voronoi diagrams (RVDs) and integrates quantities over their surface cells. We introduce Neural CVT, a learning-based surface-CVT solver that replaces these costly geometric computations with a recurrent neural optimizer, accelerating CVT optimization by one to two orders of magnitude in our benchmarks while preserving geometric fidelity. Given an input surface, we sample a dense point cloud and extract multi-scale geometric descriptors with a graph neural encoder. A lightweight recurrent optimizer then refines seed positions over a small number of iterations, aggregating interpolated surface features and optimization history to predict per-seed displacements. The framework is trained self-supervised using CVT objectives that promote uniform distributions and, when desired, feature alignment. Across diverse organic and CAD-like shapes, Neural CVT generalizes to unseen geometry, initialization strategies, and seed densities, producing isotropic, feature-preserving remeshes comparable to state-of-the-art offline optimization methods at a fraction of the computational cost. Code and trained models will be released.

</details>


### [163] [Inverse Digital Marbling: Recovering Gesture Programs with a Replay Adjoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08722)
*Tianqi Liu,Yushan Han,Hang Liu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对数字大理石纹（纸质大理石纹）模型的程序恢复方法：给定目标图像，优化一个有序的胶囊插入程序，回放后逼近目标图像。该方法利用胶囊原语的面积保持精确传输特性及闭合形式逆运算，通过回放伴随（replay adjoint）重构中间状态，在2000个手势、1024²像素下实现比检查点自动微分节省8.7倍内存，单GPU约4分钟完成拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纸质大理石纹中颜料的沉积会覆盖并置换已有的图案，使每个手势的外观与后续动作耦联。这种高度有序、非交换性的创作过程难以手工精确复现，因此需要一个可执行程序来捕捉并回放这种沉积过程，从而实现对目标图案的自动重建与可编辑表达。

Method: 将目标图像的重建建模为对有序胶囊插入（capsule insertion）程序的优化问题。胶囊原语连续连接圆形液滴与细长沉积物；其传输严格面积保持，且在沉积区域外部具有闭合形式的逆变换。利用回放伴随法重构中间状态，以保留沉积过程中丢失的坐标；并结合周期性位置检查点进一步优化。实现采用PyTorch重放与融合（fused）两种方案。

Result: 在2000手势与1024²像素下，PyTorch重放实现比检查点自动微分配置内存占用减少8.7倍且步时相当；融合实现在单个工作站GPU上约4分钟完成程序拟合。在五张大理石纹样片上评估图像重建效果，并与禁用传输的拟合、单遍几何补偿及已发表的基于描边方法（同手势数）对比。恢复的程序可在4倍线性分辨率范围内回放。按程序顺序或调色板空间指定的编辑（插入手势、重着色阶段、平移阶段）可正确回放；按图像内容指定的编辑（移动母题）则不能。合成目标上恢复程序能匹配图像但无法匹配生成手势。

Conclusion: 该方法能从目标图像恢复出可执行、可编辑的数字大理石纹程序，输出为指定数字媒介中的可编辑程序。尽管恢复的程序在图像层面达成良好重建，但无法保证恢复出与原始生成过程一致的手势序列——即多解性存在于手势层面而非图像层面。该方法具有分辨率可扩展性，且支持程序级编辑操作，为基于沉积的生成艺术提供了逆问题求解框架。

Abstract: Pigment deposition in paper marbling displaces the pattern already present, coupling the appearance of each gesture to later actions. We recover executable programs for a deposition-based digital marbling model: given a target image, we optimise an ordered program of capsule insertions whose replay approximates it. The capsule primitive continuously joins circular drops to elongated deposits. Its transport is exactly area-preserving and has a closed-form inverse on the exterior of the deposited region. A replay adjoint reconstructs intermediate states, retaining coordinates lost inside deposits and periodic position checkpoints. At 2000 gestures and 1024^2 pixels, the PyTorch replay implementation uses 8.7x less memory than the tested checkpointed-autograd configuration at comparable step time; the fused implementation fits a program in about four minutes on one workstation GPU. We evaluate image reconstruction on five marbled sheets, compare against transport-disabled fitting, one-pass geometric compensation and a published stroke-based fitter at matched stroke count, and measure sensitivity to an alternative ordered-drop transport. Recovered programs replay across a 4x range of linear resolution. Edits specified in program order or in palette space -- inserting a gesture, recolouring a stage, translating a stage -- replay correctly under the same model; edits specified by image content, such as moving a motif, do not. On synthetic targets with known generating programs, the recovered programs match the images but not the generating gestures under a positional matching statistic. The output is an editable program in the specified digital medium.

</details>
